```
1→"""Holistic investigation batch builders for review preparation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→
     7→from desloppify.intelligence.review._context.models import HolisticContext
     8→
     9→_EXTENSIONLESS_FILENAMES = {
    10→    "makefile",
    11→    "dockerfile",
    12→    "readme",
    13→    "license",
    14→    "build",
    15→    "workspace",
    16→}
    17→
    18→_GOVERNANCE_REFERENCE_FILES: tuple[str, ...] = (
    19→    "README.md",
    20→    "DEVELOPMENT_PHILOSOPHY.md",
    21→    "desloppify/README.md",
    22→    "pyproject.toml",
    23→)

    26→def _normalize_file_path(value: object) -> str | None:
    27→    """Normalize/validate candidate file paths for batch payloads."""
    28→    if not isinstance(value, str):
    29→        return None
    30→    text = value.strip().strip(",'\"")
    31→    if not text or text in {".", ".."}:
    32→        return None
    33→    if text.endswith("/"):
    34→        return None
    35→
    36→    basename = Path(text).name
    37→    if not basename:
    38→        return None
    39→    if "." not in basename and basename.lower() not in _EXTENSIONLESS_FILENAMES:
    40→        return None
    41→    return text
    42→
    43→
    44→def _collect_unique_files(
    45→    sources: list[list[dict]],
    46→    key: str = "file",
    47→    *,
    48→    max_files: int | None = None,
    49→) -> list[str]:
    50→    """Collect unique file paths from multiple source lists."""
    51→    seen: set[str] = set()
    52→    out: list[str] = []
    53→    for src in sources:
    54→        for item in src:
    55→            f = _normalize_file_path(item.get(key, ""))
    56→            if f and f not in seen:
    57→                seen.add(f)
    58→                out.append(f)
    59→                if max_files is not None and len(out) >= max_files:
    60→                    return out
    61→    return out
    62→
    63→
    64→def _existing_repo_files(
    65→    repo_root: Path | None,
    66→    candidates: tuple[str, ...],
    67→) -> list[str]:
    68→    """Return repository-relative paths for candidate files that exist."""
    69→    if repo_root is None:
    70→        return []
    71→    out: list[str] = []
    72→    for candidate in candidates:
    73→        if (repo_root / candidate).is_file():
    74→            out.append(candidate)
    75→    return out
    76→
    77→
    78→def _collect_files_from_batches(
    79→    batches: list[dict], *, max_files: int | None = None
    80→) -> list[str]:
    81→    """Collect unique file paths across batch payloads (preserving order)."""
    82→    seen: set[str] = set()
    83→    out: list[str] = []
    84→    for batch in batches:
    85→        for filepath in batch.get("files_to_read", []):
    86→            normalized = _normalize_file_path(filepath)
    87→            if not normalized:
    88→                continue
    89→            if normalized in seen:
    90→                continue
    91→            seen.add(normalized)
    92→            out.append(normalized)
    93→            if max_files is not None and len(out) >= max_files:
    94→                return out
    95→    return out
    96→
    97→
    98→def _representative_files_for_directory(
    99→    ctx: HolisticContext,
   100→    directory: str,
   101→    *,
   102→    max_files: int = 3,
   103→) -> list[str]:
   104→    """Map a directory-level signal to representative file paths."""
   105→    if not isinstance(directory, str) or not directory.strip():
   106→        return []
   107→
   108→    dir_key = directory.strip()
   109→    if dir_key in {".", "./"}:
   110→        normalized_dir = "."
   111→    else:
   112→        normalized_dir = f"{dir_key.rstrip('/')}/"
   113→
   114→    profiles = ctx.structure.get("directory_profiles", {})
   115→    profile = profiles.get(normalized_dir)
   116→    if not isinstance(profile, dict):
   117→        return []
   118→
   119→    out: list[str] = []
   120→    for filename in profile.get("files", []):
   121→        if not isinstance(filename, str) or not filename:
   122→            continue
   123→        filepath = (
   124→            filename
   125→            if normalized_dir == "."
   126→            else f"{normalized_dir.rstrip('/')}/{filename}"
   127→        )
   128→        normalized = _normalize_file_path(filepath)
   129→        if not normalized or normalized in out:
   130→            continue
   131→        out.append(normalized)
   132→        if len(out) >= max_files:
   133→            break
   134→    return out
   135→

   137→def _batch_arch_coupling(ctx: HolisticContext, *, max_files: int | None = None) -> dict:
   138→    """Batch 1: Architecture & Coupling - god modules, import-time side effects."""
   139→    files = _collect_unique_files(
   140→        [
   141→            ctx.architecture.get("god_modules", []),
   142→            ctx.coupling.get("module_level_io", []),
   143→        ],
   144→        max_files=max_files,
   145→    )

   147→        "name": "Architecture & Coupling",

   154→def _batch_conventions_errors(
   155→    ctx: HolisticContext, *, max_files: int | None = None
   156→) -> dict:
   157→    """Batch 2: Conventions & Errors - sibling behavior outliers, mixed strategies."""
   158→    sibling = ctx.conventions.get("sibling_behavior", {})
   159→    outlier_files = [
   160→        {"file": o["file"]} for di in sibling.values() for o in di.get("outliers", [])
   161→    ]
   162→    error_dirs = ctx.errors.get("strategy_by_directory", {})
   163→    mixed_dir_files: list[dict[str, str]] = []
   164→    for directory, strategies in error_dirs.items():
   165→        if not isinstance(strategies, dict) or len(strategies) < 3:
   166→            continue
   167→        for filepath in _representative_files_for_directory(ctx, directory):
   168→            mixed_dir_files.append({"file": filepath})
   169→
   170→    files = _collect_unique_files(
   171→        [outlier_files, mixed_dir_files],
   172→        max_files=max_files,
   173→    )
   174→    return {
   175→        "name": "Conventions & Errors",
   176→        "dimensions": ["convention_outlier", "error_consistency", "mid_level_elegance"],
   177→        "files_to_read": files,
   178→        "why": "naming drift, behavioral outliers, mixed error strategies",
   179→    }

   186→    util_files = ctx.abstractions.get("util_files", [])
   187→    wrapper_files = [

   202→    interface_files: list[dict[str, str]] = []
   203→    for item in ctx.abstractions.get("one_impl_interfaces", []):

   206→        for group in ("declared_in", "implemented_in"):
   207→            for filepath in item.get(group, []):
   208→                interface_files.append({"file": filepath})
   209→
   210→    cycle_files: list[dict] = []
   211→    for summary in ctx.dependencies.get("cycle_summaries", []):
   212→        for token in summary.split():
   213→            if "/" in token and "." in token:
   214→                cycle_files.append({"file": token.strip(",'\"")})
   215→    files = _collect_unique_files(
   216→        [
   217→            util_files,
   218→            wrapper_files,

   220→            param_bag_files,

   226→    return {

   230→            "dependency_health",
   231→            "mid_level_elegance",
   232→            "low_level_elegance",
   233→        ],
   234→        "files_to_read": files,
   235→        "why": "abstraction hotspots (wrappers/interfaces/param bags), dep cycles",
   236→    }

   239→def _batch_testing_api(ctx: HolisticContext, *, max_files: int | None = None) -> dict:

   252→def _batch_authorization(ctx: HolisticContext, *, max_files: int | None = None) -> dict:

   254→    auth_ctx = ctx.authorization

   270→def _batch_ai_debt_migrations(
   271→    ctx: HolisticContext, *, max_files: int | None = None
   272→) -> dict:
   273→    """Batch 6: AI Debt & Migrations - deprecated markers, migration TODOs."""
   274→    ai_debt = ctx.ai_debt_signals
   275→    migration = ctx.migration_signals
   276→    debt_files: list[dict] = []
   277→    for rpath in ai_debt.get("file_signals", {}):

   290→            "incomplete_migration",
   291→            "low_level_elegance",

   293→        "files_to_read": files,

   297→
   298→def _batch_package_organization(
   299→    ctx: HolisticContext, *, max_files: int | None = None
   300→) -> dict:
   301→    """Batch 7: Package Organization - file placement, directory boundaries."""
   302→    structure = ctx.structure
   303→    struct_files: list[dict] = []
   304→    for rf in structure.get("root_files", []):
   305→        if rf.get("role") == "peripheral":
   306→            struct_files.append({"file": rf["file"]})
   307→    dir_profiles = structure.get("directory_profiles", {})
   308→    largest_dirs = sorted(
   309→        dir_profiles.items(), key=lambda x: -x[1].get("file_count", 0)
   310→    )[:3]
   311→    for dir_key, profile in largest_dirs:
   312→        for fname in profile.get("files", [])[:3]:
   313→            dir_path = dir_key.rstrip("/")
   314→            rpath = f"{dir_path}/{fname}" if dir_path != "." else fname
   315→            struct_files.append({"file": rpath})
   316→    coupling_matrix = structure.get("coupling_matrix", {})
   317→    seen_edges: set[str] = set()
   318→    for edge in coupling_matrix:
   319→        if " → " in edge:
   320→            a, b = edge.split(" → ", 1)
   321→            reverse = f"{b} → {a}"
   322→            if reverse in coupling_matrix and edge not in seen_edges:
   323→                seen_edges.add(edge)
   324→                seen_edges.add(reverse)
   325→                for d in (a, b):
   326→                    for fname in dir_profiles.get(d, {}).get("files", [])[:2]:
   327→                        dir_path = d.rstrip("/")
   328→                        rpath = f"{dir_path}/{fname}" if dir_path != "." else fname
   329→                        struct_files.append({"file": rpath})
   330→    files = _collect_unique_files([struct_files], max_files=max_files)
   331→    return {
   332→        "name": "Package Organization",
   333→        "dimensions": ["package_organization", "high_level_elegance"],
   334→        "files_to_read": files,
   335→        "why": "file placement, directory boundaries, architectural layering",
   336→    }
   337→
   338→
   339→def _batch_governance_contracts(
   340→    ctx: HolisticContext,
   341→    *,
   342→    repo_root: Path | None,
   343→    max_files: int | None = None,
   344→) -> dict:
   345→    """Batch 8: Governance & Contracts - docs/policy promises vs runtime posture."""
   346→    docs = _existing_repo_files(repo_root, _GOVERNANCE_REFERENCE_FILES)
   347→    if not docs:
   348→        return {

   358→        }
   359→    top_imported = [
   360→        {"file": filepath}

   362→        if isinstance(filepath, str)
   363→    ]
   364→    anchor_files = _collect_unique_files(

   368→            ctx.coupling.get("module_level_io", []),
   369→        ],
   370→        max_files=5,
   371→    )
   372→    seen = set(docs)
   373→    files = list(docs)
   374→    for filepath in anchor_files:
   375→        if filepath in seen:
   376→            continue
   377→        seen.add(filepath)
   378→        files.append(filepath)
   379→    if max_files is not None:
   380→        files = files[:max_files]
   381→    return {
   382→        "name": "Governance & Contracts",
   383→        "dimensions": [
   384→            "cross_module_architecture",
   385→            "high_level_elegance",
   386→            "test_strategy",
   387→            "package_organization",

   389→        "files_to_read": files,

   394→def _ensure_holistic_context(holistic_ctx: HolisticContext | dict) -> HolisticContext:
   395→    if isinstance(holistic_ctx, HolisticContext):
   396→        return holistic_ctx
   397→    return HolisticContext.from_raw(holistic_ctx)
   398→
   399→
   400→def build_investigation_batches(
   401→    holistic_ctx: HolisticContext | dict,
   402→    lang: object,
   403→    *,
   404→    repo_root: Path | None = None,
   405→    max_files_per_batch: int | None = None,
   406→) -> list[dict]:
   407→    """Derive parallelizable investigation batches from holistic context."""
   408→    ctx = _ensure_holistic_context(holistic_ctx)
   409→    del lang  # Reserved for future language-specific batch shaping.
   410→    batches = [
   411→        _batch_arch_coupling(ctx, max_files=max_files_per_batch),
   412→        _batch_conventions_errors(ctx, max_files=max_files_per_batch),
   413→        _batch_abstractions_deps(ctx, max_files=max_files_per_batch),
   414→        _batch_testing_api(ctx, max_files=max_files_per_batch),
   415→        _batch_authorization(ctx, max_files=max_files_per_batch),
   416→        _batch_ai_debt_migrations(ctx, max_files=max_files_per_batch),
   417→        _batch_package_organization(ctx, max_files=max_files_per_batch),
   418→        _batch_governance_contracts(
   419→            ctx,
   420→            repo_root=repo_root,
   421→            max_files=max_files_per_batch,
   422→        ),
   423→    ]
   424→    return [batch for batch in batches if batch["files_to_read"]]
   425→
   426→
   427→def filter_batches_to_dimensions(
   428→    batches: list[dict],
   429→    dimensions: list[str],
   430→    *,
   431→    fallback_max_files: int | None = 80,
   432→) -> list[dict]:
   433→    """Keep only dimensions explicitly active for this holistic review run.
   434→
   435→    If selected dimensions are not represented by any batch mapping, append a
   436→    fallback batch over representative files so scoped runs still get guidance.
   437→    """
   438→    selected = [d for d in dimensions if isinstance(d, str) and d]
   439→    if not selected:
   440→        return []
   441→    selected_set = set(selected)
   442→    filtered: list[dict] = []
   443→    covered: set[str] = set()
   444→    for batch in batches:
   445→        batch_dims = [dim for dim in batch.get("dimensions", []) if dim in selected_set]
   446→        if not batch_dims:
   447→            continue
   448→        filtered.append({**batch, "dimensions": batch_dims})
   449→        covered.update(batch_dims)
   450→
   451→    missing = [dim for dim in selected if dim not in covered]
   452→    if not missing:
   453→        return filtered
   454→
   455→    # Keep fallback batches tractable; giant sweeps are expensive and often
   456→    # unnecessary when dimensions are already explicitly scoped.
   457→    max_files = fallback_max_files if isinstance(fallback_max_files, int) else None
   458→    if isinstance(max_files, int) and max_files <= 0:
   459→        max_files = None
   460→    fallback_files = _collect_files_from_batches(filtered or batches, max_files=max_files)
   461→    if not fallback_files:
   462→        return filtered
   463→
   464→    filtered.append(
   465→        {
   466→            "name": "Cross-cutting Sweep",
   467→            "dimensions": missing,
   468→            "files_to_read": fallback_files,
   469→            "why": "selected dimensions had no direct batch mapping; review representative cross-cutting files",
   470→        }
   471→    )
   472→    return filtered
   473→
   474→
   475→def batch_concerns(
   476→    concerns: list,
   477→    *,
   478→    max_files: int | None = None,
   479→) -> dict | None:
   480→    """Build investigation batch from mechanical concern signals.
   481→
   482→    *concerns* should be a list of Concern dataclass instances from
   483→    ``desloppify.engine.concerns``.
   484→    """
   485→    if not concerns:
   486→        return None
   487→    types = sorted({c.type for c in concerns if c.type})
   488→    why_parts = ["mechanical detectors identified structural patterns needing judgment"]
   489→    if types:
   490→        why_parts.append(f"concern types: {', '.join(types)}")
   491→    files: list[str] = []
   492→    seen: set[str] = set()
   493→    for concern in concerns:
   494→        candidate = _normalize_file_path(getattr(concern, "file", ""))
   495→        if not candidate or candidate in seen:
   496→            continue
   497→        seen.add(candidate)
   498→        files.append(candidate)
   499→
   500→    total_candidate_files = len(files)
   501→    if (
   502→        max_files is not None
   503→        and isinstance(max_files, int)
   504→        and max_files > 0
   505→        and total_candidate_files > max_files
   506→    ):
   507→        files = files[:max_files]
   508→        why_parts.append(
   509→            f"truncated to {max_files} files from {total_candidate_files} candidates"
   510→        )
   511→
   512→    return {
   513→        "name": "Design coherence — Mechanical Concern Signals",
   514→        "dimensions": ["design_coherence"],
   515→        "files_to_read": files,
   516→        "why": "; ".join(why_parts),
   517→        "total_candidate_files": total_candidate_files,
   518→    }
   519→
   520→
   521→__all__ = ["batch_concerns", "build_investigation_batches", "filter_batches_to_dimensions"]
   522→
```

```
   100→def _collect_reviewed_files_from_batches(
   101→    *,
   102→    batches: list[dict[str, object]],
   103→    selected_indexes: list[int],
   104→) -> list[str]:
   105→    """Collect normalized file paths reviewed in the selected batch set."""
   106→    reviewed: list[str] = []
   107→    seen: set[str] = set()
   108→    for idx in selected_indexes:
   109→        if idx < 0 or idx >= len(batches):
   110→            continue
   111→        batch = batches[idx]
   112→        files = batch.get("files_to_read", [])
   113→        if not isinstance(files, list):
   114→            continue
   115→        for raw in files:
   116→            if not isinstance(raw, str):
   117→                continue
   118→            path = raw.strip().strip(",'\"")
   119→            if not path or path in {".", ".."}:
   120→                continue
   121→            if path.endswith("/"):
   122→                continue
   123→            if path in seen:
   124→                continue
   125→            seen.add(path)
   126→            reviewed.append(path)
   127→    return reviewed

   169→def _normalize_dimension_list(raw: object) -> list[str]:
   170→    """Normalize dimension collections to a stable, de-duplicated list."""
   171→    if not isinstance(raw, list):

   173→    out: list[str] = []

   175→    for item in raw:

   197→    selected_dims: list[str],
   198→    scored_dims: list[str],
   199→) -> list[str]:
   200→    selected = set(selected_dims)
   201→    return [dim for dim in scored_dims if dim not in selected]
   202→
   203→
   204→def _missing_dimensions_command(*, missing_dims: list[str], scan_path: str) -> str:
   205→    """Return rerun command for missing subjective dimensions."""
   206→    base = "desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import"
   207→    if scan_path and scan_path != ".":
   208→        base += f" --path {shlex.quote(scan_path)}"
   209→    if missing_dims:
   210→        base += f" --dimensions {','.join(missing_dims)}"
   211→    return base
   212→
   213→
   214→def _print_preflight_dimension_scope_notice(
   215→    *,
   216→    selected_dims: list[str],
   217→    scored_dims: list[str],
   218→    explicit_selection: bool,
   219→    scan_path: str,
   220→    colorize_fn,
   221→) -> None:
   222→    """Print trigger-time notice when run scope is a scored-dimension subset."""
   223→    if not scored_dims:
   224→        return
   225→    missing_dims = _missing_scored_dimensions(
   226→        selected_dims=selected_dims,
   227→        scored_dims=scored_dims,
   228→    )
   229→    if not missing_dims:
   230→        return
   231→
   232→    covered_count = len([dim for dim in selected_dims if dim in set(scored_dims)])
   233→    scope_reason = (
   234→        "explicit --dimensions selection"
   235→        if explicit_selection
   236→        else "language default review dimension set"
   237→    )
   238→    tone = "yellow" if explicit_selection else "red"
   239→    print(
   240→        colorize_fn(
   241→            "  WARNING: this run targets "
   242→            f"{covered_count}/{len(scored_dims)} scored subjective dimensions "
   243→            f"({scope_reason}).",
   244→            tone,
   245→        )
   246→    )
   247→    preview = ", ".join(missing_dims[:5])
   248→    if len(missing_dims) > 5:
   249→        preview = f"{preview}, +{len(missing_dims) - 5} more"
   250→    print(colorize_fn(f"  Missing from this run: {preview}", "yellow"))
   251→    print(
   252→        colorize_fn(
   253→            "  Rerun missing dimensions: "
   254→            f"`{_missing_dimensions_command(missing_dims=missing_dims, scan_path=scan_path)}`",
   255→            "dim",
   256→        )
   257→    )
   258→
   259→
   260→def _print_import_dimension_coverage_notice(
   261→    *,
   262→    assessed_dims: list[str],
   263→    scored_dims: list[str],
   264→    scan_path: str,
   265→    colorize_fn,
   266→) -> list[str]:
   267→    """Print result-time notice when merged import covers only a subset."""
   268→    if not scored_dims:
   269→        return []
   270→    missing_dims = _missing_scored_dimensions(
   271→        selected_dims=assessed_dims,
   272→        scored_dims=scored_dims,
   273→    )
   274→    if not missing_dims:
   275→        return []
   276→
   277→    covered_count = len([dim for dim in assessed_dims if dim in set(scored_dims)])
   278→    print(
   279→        colorize_fn(
   280→            "  Coverage gap: imported assessments for "
   281→            f"{covered_count}/{len(scored_dims)} scored subjective dimensions.",
   282→            "yellow",
   283→        )
   284→    )
   285→    preview = ", ".join(missing_dims[:5])
   286→    if len(missing_dims) > 5:
   287→        preview = f"{preview}, +{len(missing_dims) - 5} more"
   288→    print(colorize_fn(f"  Still missing: {preview}", "yellow"))
   289→    print(
   290→        colorize_fn(
   291→            "  Run to cover missing dimensions: "
   292→            f"`{_missing_dimensions_command(missing_dims=missing_dims, scan_path=scan_path)}`",
   293→            "dim",
   294→        )
   295→    )
   296→    return missing_dims
   297→
   298→
   299→def do_run_batches(
   300→    args,
   301→    state,
   302→    lang,
   303→    state_file,
   304→    *,
   305→    config: dict | None,
   306→    run_stamp_fn,
   307→    load_or_prepare_packet_fn,
   308→    selected_batch_indexes_fn,
   309→    prepare_run_artifacts_fn,
   310→    run_codex_batch_fn,
   311→    execute_batches_fn,
   312→    collect_batch_results_fn,
   313→    print_failures_fn,
   314→    print_failures_and_exit_fn,
   315→    merge_batch_results_fn,
   316→    build_import_provenance_fn,
   317→    do_import_fn,
   318→    run_followup_scan_fn,
   319→    safe_write_text_fn,
   320→    colorize_fn,
   321→    project_root: Path,
   322→    subagent_runs_dir: Path,
   323→) -> None:
   324→    """Run holistic investigation batches with a local subagent runner."""
   325→    config = config or {}
   326→    runner = getattr(args, "runner", "codex")
   327→    _validate_runner(runner, colorize_fn=colorize_fn)
   328→    allow_partial = bool(getattr(args, "allow_partial", False))
   329→    run_parallel = bool(getattr(args, "parallel", False))
   330→    max_parallel_batches = _coerce_positive_int(
   331→        getattr(args, "max_parallel_batches", None),
   332→        default=3,
   333→        minimum=1,
   334→    )
   335→    heartbeat_seconds = _coerce_positive_float(
   336→        getattr(args, "batch_heartbeat_seconds", None),
   337→        default=15.0,
   338→        minimum=0.1,
   339→    )
   340→    batch_timeout_seconds = _coerce_positive_int(
   341→        getattr(args, "batch_timeout_seconds", None),
   342→        default=2 * 60 * 60,
   343→        minimum=1,
   344→    )
   345→    batch_max_retries = _coerce_positive_int(
   346→        getattr(args, "batch_max_retries", None),
   347→        default=1,
   348→        minimum=0,
   349→    )
   350→    batch_retry_backoff_seconds = _coerce_non_negative_float(
   351→        getattr(args, "batch_retry_backoff_seconds", None),
   352→        default=2.0,
   353→    )
   354→    raw_stall_warning = getattr(args, "batch_stall_warning_seconds", None)
   355→    stall_warning_seconds = 0
   356→    if isinstance(raw_stall_warning, int | float | str):
   357→        try:
   358→            parsed_stall_warning = int(raw_stall_warning)
   359→        except (TypeError, ValueError):
   360→            parsed_stall_warning = 0
   361→        stall_warning_seconds = parsed_stall_warning if parsed_stall_warning > 0 else 0
   362→    raw_stall_kill = getattr(args, "batch_stall_kill_seconds", None)
   363→    stall_kill_seconds = 0
   364→    if isinstance(raw_stall_kill, int | float | str):
   365→        try:
   366→            parsed_stall_kill = int(raw_stall_kill)
   367→        except (TypeError, ValueError):
   368→            parsed_stall_kill = 0
   369→        stall_kill_seconds = parsed_stall_kill if parsed_stall_kill > 0 else 0
   370→
   371→    stamp = run_stamp_fn()
   372→    packet, immutable_packet_path, prompt_packet_path = load_or_prepare_packet_fn(
   373→        args,
   374→        state=state,
   375→        lang=lang,
   376→        config=config,
   377→        stamp=stamp,
   378→    )
   379→
   380→    scan_path = str(getattr(args, "path", ".") or ".")
   381→    packet_dimensions = _normalize_dimension_list(packet.get("dimensions", []))
   382→    scored_dimensions = _scored_dimensions_for_lang(lang.name)
   383→    _print_preflight_dimension_scope_notice(
   384→        selected_dims=packet_dimensions,
   385→        scored_dims=scored_dimensions,
   386→        explicit_selection=bool(getattr(args, "dimensions", None)),
   387→        scan_path=scan_path,
   388→        colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   389→    )
   390→    suggested_prepare_cmd = f"desloppify review --prepare --path {scan_path}"
   391→    batches = _require_batches(
   392→        packet,
   393→        colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   394→        suggested_prepare_cmd=suggested_prepare_cmd,
   395→    )
   396→
   397→    selected_indexes = selected_batch_indexes_fn(args, batch_count=len(batches))
   398→    total_batches = len(selected_indexes)
   399→    effective_workers = min(total_batches, max_parallel_batches) if run_parallel else 1
   400→    waves = max(1, math.ceil(total_batches / max(1, effective_workers)))
   401→    worst_case_seconds = waves * batch_timeout_seconds
   402→    worst_case_minutes = max(1, math.ceil(worst_case_seconds / 60))
   403→    print(
   404→        colorize_fn(
   405→            "  Runtime expectation: "
   406→            f"{total_batches} batch(es), workers={effective_workers}, "
   407→            f"timeout-per-batch={int(batch_timeout_seconds / 60)}m, "
   408→            f"worst-case upper bound ~{worst_case_minutes}m.",
   409→            "dim",
   410→        )
   411→    )
   412→    run_dir, logs_dir, prompt_files, output_files, log_files = prepare_run_artifacts_fn(
   413→        stamp=stamp,
   414→        selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   415→        batches=batches,
   416→        packet_path=prompt_packet_path,
   417→        run_root=subagent_runs_dir,
   418→        repo_root=project_root,
   419→    )
   420→    raw_run_log_file = getattr(args, "run_log_file", None)
   421→    run_log_file_value = (
   422→        raw_run_log_file.strip()
   423→        if isinstance(raw_run_log_file, str) and raw_run_log_file.strip()
   424→        else None
   425→    )
   426→    if run_log_file_value is not None:
   427→        candidate = Path(run_log_file_value).expanduser()
   428→        run_log_path = candidate if candidate.is_absolute() else project_root / candidate
   429→    else:
   430→        run_log_path = run_dir / "run.log"
   431→    run_log_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   432→
   433→    def _append_run_log(message: str) -> None:
   434→        line = f"{datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec='seconds')} {message}\n"
   435→        try:
   436→            with run_log_path.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
   437→                handle.write(line)
   438→        except OSError:
   439→            return
   440→
   441→    _append_run_log(
   442→        "run-start "
   443→        f"runner={runner} parallel={run_parallel} max_parallel={max_parallel_batches} "
   444→        f"timeout={batch_timeout_seconds}s heartbeat={heartbeat_seconds:.1f}s "
   445→        f"stall_warning={stall_warning_seconds}s stall_kill={stall_kill_seconds}s "
   446→        f"retries={batch_max_retries} "
   447→        f"retry_backoff={batch_retry_backoff_seconds:.1f}s upper_bound={worst_case_minutes}m "
   448→        f"selected={[idx + 1 for idx in selected_indexes]}"
   449→    )
   450→    _append_run_log(f"run-path {run_dir}")
   451→    _append_run_log(f"packet {immutable_packet_path}")
   452→    _append_run_log(f"blind-packet {prompt_packet_path}")
   453→    print(colorize_fn(f"  Live run log: {run_log_path}", "dim"))
   454→
   455→    if getattr(args, "dry_run", False):
   456→        print(
   457→            colorize_fn(
   458→                "  Dry run only: prompts generated, runner execution skipped.", "yellow"
   459→            )
   460→        )
   461→        print(colorize_fn(f"  Immutable packet: {immutable_packet_path}", "dim"))
   462→        print(colorize_fn(f"  Blind packet: {prompt_packet_path}", "dim"))
   463→        print(colorize_fn(f"  Prompts: {run_dir / 'prompts'}", "dim"))
   464→        _append_run_log("run-finished dry-run")
   465→        return
   466→
   467→    def _run_batch(*, prompt: str, output_file: Path, log_file: Path) -> int:
   468→        return run_codex_batch_fn(
   469→            prompt=prompt,
   470→            repo_root=project_root,
   471→            output_file=output_file,
   472→            log_file=log_file,
   473→        )
   474→
   475→    batch_positions = {batch_idx: pos + 1 for pos, batch_idx in enumerate(selected_indexes)}
   476→    summary_created_at = datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   477→    stall_warned_batches: set[int] = set()
   478→    batch_status: dict[str, dict[str, object]] = {
   479→        str(idx + 1): {
   480→            "position": batch_positions.get(idx, 0),
   481→            "status": "pending",
   482→            "prompt_path": str(prompt_files[idx]),
   483→            "result_path": str(output_files[idx]),
   484→            "log_path": str(log_files[idx]),
   485→        }
   486→        for idx in selected_indexes
   487→    }
   488→
   489→    if run_parallel:
   490→        print(
   491→            colorize_fn(
   492→                "  Parallel runner config: "
   493→                f"max-workers={min(total_batches, max_parallel_batches)}, "
   494→                f"heartbeat={heartbeat_seconds:.1f}s",
   495→                "dim",
   496→            )
   497→        )
   498→
   499→    def _report_progress(
   500→        batch_index: int,
   501→        event: str,
   502→        code: int | None = None,
   503→        **details,
   504→    ) -> None:
   505→        if event == "heartbeat":
   506→            active = details.get("active_batches")
   507→            queued = details.get("queued_batches", [])
   508→            elapsed = details.get("elapsed_seconds", {})
   509→            if not isinstance(active, list):
   510→                active = []
   511→            if not isinstance(queued, list):
   512→                queued = []
   513→            if not active and not queued:
   514→                return
   515→            segments: list[str] = []
   516→            for idx in active[:6]:
   517→                secs = 0
   518→                if isinstance(elapsed, dict):
   519→                    raw_secs = elapsed.get(idx, 0)
   520→                    secs = int(raw_secs) if isinstance(raw_secs, int | float) else 0
   521→                segments.append(f"#{idx + 1}:{secs}s")
   522→            if len(active) > 6:
   523→                segments.append(f"+{len(active) - 6} more")
   524→            queued_segment = ""
   525→            if queued:
   526→                queued_segment = f", queued {len(queued)}"
   527→            print(
   528→                colorize_fn(
   529→                    "  Batch heartbeat: "
   530→                    f"{len(active)}/{total_batches} active{queued_segment} "
   531→                    f"({', '.join(segments) if segments else 'running batches pending'})",
   532→                    "dim",
   533→                )
   534→            )
   535→            _append_run_log(
   536→                "heartbeat "
   537→                f"active={[idx + 1 for idx in active]} queued={[idx + 1 for idx in queued]} "
   538→                f"elapsed={{{', '.join(f'{idx + 1}:{elapsed.get(idx, 0)}' for idx in active)}}}"
   539→            )
   540→            if stall_warning_seconds > 0 and isinstance(elapsed, dict):
   541→                slow_active = [
   542→                    idx
   543→                    for idx in active
   544→                    if isinstance(elapsed.get(idx), int | float)
   545→                    and int(elapsed.get(idx) or 0) >= stall_warning_seconds
   546→                ]
   547→                newly_warned = [idx for idx in slow_active if idx not in stall_warned_batches]
   548→                if newly_warned:
   549→                    stall_warned_batches.update(newly_warned)
   550→                    warning_message = (
   551→                        "  Stall warning: batches "
   552→                        f"{[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(newly_warned)]} exceeded "
   553→                        f"{stall_warning_seconds}s elapsed. "
   554→                        "This may be normal for long runs; review run.log and batch logs."
   555→                    )
   556→                    print(colorize_fn(warning_message, "yellow"))
   557→                    _append_run_log(
   558→                        "stall-warning "
   559→                        f"threshold={stall_warning_seconds}s batches={[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(newly_warned)]}"
   560→                    )
   561→            return
   562→
   563→        position = batch_positions.get(batch_index, 0)
   564→        key = str(batch_index + 1)
   565→        state = batch_status.setdefault(
   566→            key,
   567→            {
   568→                "position": position,
   569→                "status": "pending",
   570→                "prompt_path": str(prompt_files.get(batch_index, "")),
   571→                "result_path": str(output_files.get(batch_index, "")),
   572→                "log_path": str(log_files.get(batch_index, "")),
   573→            },
   574→        )
   575→        if event == "queued":
   576→            state["status"] = "queued"
   577→            print(
   578→                colorize_fn(
   579→                    f"  Batch {position}/{total_batches} queued (#{batch_index + 1})",
   580→                    "dim",
   581→                )
   582→            )
   583→            _append_run_log(f"batch-queued batch={batch_index + 1} position={position}/{total_batches}")
   584→            return
   585→        if event == "start":
   586→            state["status"] = "running"
   587→            state["started_at"] = datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   588→            print(
   589→                colorize_fn(
   590→                    f"  Batch {position}/{total_batches} started (#{batch_index + 1})",
   591→                    "dim",
   592→                )
   593→            )
   594→            _append_run_log(f"batch-start batch={batch_index + 1} position={position}/{total_batches}")
   595→            return
   596→        if event == "done":
   597→            status = "done" if code == 0 else f"failed ({code})"
   598→            tone = "dim" if code == 0 else "yellow"
   599→            elapsed_seconds = details.get("elapsed_seconds")
   600→            elapsed_suffix = ""
   601→            if isinstance(elapsed_seconds, int | float):
   602→                elapsed_suffix = f" in {int(max(0, elapsed_seconds))}s"
   603→                state["elapsed_seconds"] = int(max(0, elapsed_seconds))
   604→            state["status"] = "succeeded" if code == 0 else "failed"
   605→            state["exit_code"] = int(code) if isinstance(code, int) else code
   606→            state["completed_at"] = datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   607→            if batch_index in stall_warned_batches:
   608→                stall_warned_batches.discard(batch_index)
   609→            print(
   610→                colorize_fn(
   611→                    f"  Batch {position}/{total_batches} {status}{elapsed_suffix} (#{batch_index + 1})",
   612→                    tone,
   613→                )
   614→            )
   615→            _append_run_log(
   616→                f"batch-done batch={batch_index + 1} position={position}/{total_batches} "
   617→                f"code={code} elapsed={state.get('elapsed_seconds', 0)}"
   618→            )
   619→
   620→    execution_failures = execute_batches_fn(
   621→        selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   622→        prompt_files=prompt_files,
   623→        output_files=output_files,
   624→        log_files=log_files,
   625→        run_parallel=run_parallel,
   626→        run_batch_fn=_run_batch,
   627→        safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text_fn,
   628→        progress_fn=_report_progress,
   629→        max_parallel_workers=max_parallel_batches,
   630→        heartbeat_seconds=heartbeat_seconds,
   631→    )
   632→
   633→    allowed_dims = {
   634→        str(dim) for dim in packet.get("dimensions", []) if isinstance(dim, str)
   635→    }
   636→    batch_results, failures = collect_batch_results_fn(
   637→        selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   638→        failures=execution_failures,
   639→        output_files=output_files,
   640→        allowed_dims=allowed_dims,
   641→    )
   642→
   643→    execution_failure_set = set(execution_failures)
   644→    failure_set = set(failures)
   645→    successful_indexes = sorted(idx for idx in selected_indexes if idx not in failure_set)
   646→    for idx in selected_indexes:
   647→        key = str(idx + 1)
   648→        state = batch_status.setdefault(
   649→            key,
   650→            {"position": batch_positions.get(idx, 0), "status": "pending"},
   651→        )
   652→        if idx not in failure_set:
   653→            state["status"] = "succeeded"
   654→            continue
   655→        if idx in execution_failure_set:
   656→            state["status"] = "failed"
   657→            continue
   658→        if not output_files[idx].exists():
   659→            state["status"] = "missing_output"
   660→            continue
   661→        state["status"] = "parse_failed"
   662→
   663→    run_summary = {
   664→        "created_at": summary_created_at,
   665→        "run_stamp": stamp,
   666→        "runner": runner,
   667→        "parallel": run_parallel,
   668→        "selected_batches": [idx + 1 for idx in selected_indexes],
   669→        "successful_batches": [idx + 1 for idx in successful_indexes],
   670→        "failed_batches": [idx + 1 for idx in sorted(failure_set)],
   671→        "allow_partial": allow_partial,
   672→        "max_parallel_batches": max_parallel_batches if run_parallel else 1,
   673→        "batch_timeout_seconds": batch_timeout_seconds,
   674→        "batch_max_retries": batch_max_retries,
   675→        "batch_retry_backoff_seconds": batch_retry_backoff_seconds,
   676→        "batch_heartbeat_seconds": heartbeat_seconds if run_parallel else None,
   677→        "batch_stall_warning_seconds": stall_warning_seconds if run_parallel else None,
   678→        "batch_stall_kill_seconds": stall_kill_seconds,
   679→        "immutable_packet": str(immutable_packet_path),
   680→        "blind_packet": str(prompt_packet_path),
   681→        "run_dir": str(run_dir),
   682→        "logs_dir": str(logs_dir),
   683→        "run_log": str(run_log_path),
   684→        "batches": batch_status,
   685→    }
   686→    run_summary_path = run_dir / "run_summary.json"
   687→    safe_write_text_fn(run_summary_path, json.dumps(run_summary, indent=2) + "\n")
   688→    print(colorize_fn(f"  Run summary: {run_summary_path}", "dim"))
   689→    _append_run_log(f"run-summary {run_summary_path}")
   690→
   691→    if failures and (not allow_partial or not batch_results):
   692→        _append_run_log(
   693→            f"run-finished failures={[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(failure_set)]} mode=exit"
   694→        )
   695→        print_failures_and_exit_fn(
   696→            failures=failures,
   697→            packet_path=immutable_packet_path,
   698→            logs_dir=logs_dir,
   699→            colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   700→        )
   701→    elif failures:
   702→        print(
   703→            colorize_fn(
   704→                "  Partial completion enabled: importing successful batches and keeping failed batches open.",
   705→                "yellow",
   706→            )
   707→        )
   708→        print_failures_fn(
   709→            failures=failures,
   710→            packet_path=immutable_packet_path,
   711→            logs_dir=logs_dir,
   712→            colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   713→        )
   714→        _append_run_log(
   715→            "run-partial "
   716→            f"successful={[idx + 1 for idx in successful_indexes]} "
   717→            f"failed={[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(failure_set)]}"
   718→        )
   719→
   720→    merged = merge_batch_results_fn(batch_results)
   721→    reviewed_files = _collect_reviewed_files_from_batches(
   722→        batches=batches,
   723→        selected_indexes=successful_indexes,
   724→    )
   725→    full_sweep_included = any(
   726→        str(batch.get("name", "")).strip().lower() == "full codebase sweep"
   727→        for idx in successful_indexes
   728→        if 0 <= idx < len(batches)
   729→        for batch in [batches[idx]]
   730→        if isinstance(batch, dict)
   731→    )
   732→    review_scope: dict[str, object] = {
   733→        "reviewed_files_count": len(reviewed_files),
   734→        "successful_batch_count": len(successful_indexes),
   735→        "full_sweep_included": full_sweep_included,
   736→    }
   737→    total_files = packet.get("total_files")
   738→    if isinstance(total_files, int) and not isinstance(total_files, bool) and total_files > 0:
   739→        review_scope["total_files"] = total_files
   740→    merged["review_scope"] = review_scope
   741→    if reviewed_files:
   742→        merged["reviewed_files"] = reviewed_files
   743→        print(
   744→            colorize_fn(
   745→                f"  Reviewed files captured for cache refresh: {len(reviewed_files)}",
   746→                "dim",
   747→            )
   748→        )
   749→    merged["provenance"] = build_import_provenance_fn(
   750→        runner=runner,
   751→        blind_packet_path=prompt_packet_path,
   752→        run_stamp=stamp,
   753→        batch_indexes=successful_indexes,
   754→    )
   755→    merged_assessment_dims = _normalize_dimension_list(
   756→        list((merged.get("assessments") or {}).keys())
   757→    )
   758→    merged_finding_dims = _normalize_dimension_list(
   759→        [
   760→            finding.get("dimension")
   761→            for finding in (merged.get("findings") or [])
   762→            if isinstance(finding, dict)
   763→        ]
   764→    )
   765→    merged_imported_dims = _normalize_dimension_list(
   766→        merged_assessment_dims + merged_finding_dims
   767→    )
   768→    review_scope["imported_dimensions"] = merged_imported_dims
   769→    missing_after_import = _print_import_dimension_coverage_notice(
   770→        assessed_dims=merged_assessment_dims,
   771→        scored_dims=scored_dimensions,
   772→        scan_path=scan_path,
   773→        colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   774→    )
   775→    merged["assessment_coverage"] = {
   776→        "scored_dimensions": scored_dimensions,
   777→        "selected_dimensions": packet_dimensions,
   778→        "imported_dimensions": merged_assessment_dims,
   779→        "missing_dimensions": missing_after_import,
   780→    }
   781→    merged_path = run_dir / "holistic_findings_merged.json"
   782→    safe_write_text_fn(merged_path, json.dumps(merged, indent=2) + "\n")
   783→    print(colorize_fn(f"\n  Merged outputs: {merged_path}", "bold"))
   784→    _print_review_quality(merged.get("review_quality", {}), colorize_fn=colorize_fn)
   785→
   786→    try:
   787→        do_import_fn(
   788→            str(merged_path),
   789→            state,
   790→            lang,
   791→            state_file,
   792→            config=config,
   793→            allow_partial=allow_partial,
   794→            trusted_assessment_source=True,
   795→            trusted_assessment_label="trusted internal run-batches import",
   796→        )
   797→    except SystemExit as exc:
   798→        _append_run_log(f"run-finished import-failed code={exc.code}")
   799→        raise
   800→    except Exception as exc:
   801→        _append_run_log(f"run-finished import-error error={exc}")
   802→        raise
   803→    _append_run_log(
   804→        "run-finished "
   805→        f"successful={[idx + 1 for idx in successful_indexes]} "
   806→        f"failed={[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(failure_set)]} imported={str(merged_path)}"
   807→    )
   808→
   809→    if getattr(args, "scan_after_import", False):
   810→        followup_code = run_followup_scan_fn(
   811→            lang_name=lang.name,
   812→            scan_path=str(args.path),
   813→        )
   814→        if followup_code != 0:
   815→            print(
   816→                colorize_fn(
   817→                    f"  Follow-up scan failed with exit code {followup_code}.",
   818→                    "red",
   819→                ),
   820→                file=sys.stderr,
   821→            )
   822→            raise SystemExit(followup_code)
   823→
   824→
   825→__all__ = ["do_run_batches"]
   826→
```

```
    40→_ISSUES_STATUS_LIKE = {
    41→    "open",
    42→    "fixed",

    44→    "false_positive",
    45→    "auto_resolved",
    46→    "resolved",

    51→def _issues_state_file(value: str) -> str:
    52→    """Validate issues state-path inputs and reject obvious status-like tokens."""
    53→    candidate = str(value).strip()
    54→    normalized = candidate.lower()
    55→    local_candidate = Path(candidate)
    56→
    57→    if (
    58→        normalized in _ISSUES_STATUS_LIKE
    59→        and local_candidate.suffix == ""
    60→        and not local_candidate.exists()
    61→        and local_candidate.parent == Path(".")
    62→    ):
    63→        raise argparse.ArgumentTypeError(
    64→            f"'{candidate}' looks like a status value, but `issues --state-file` expects "
    65→            "a path to a state JSON file. Use `desloppify issues list` and pass "
    66→            "a file path such as `.desloppify/state-typescript.json` when needed."
    67→        )
    68→
    69→    return candidate

   451→def _fixer_help_lines(langs: list[str]) -> list[str]:
   452→    fixer_help_lines: list[str] = []
   453→    for lang_name in langs:
   454→        try:
   455→            fixer_names = sorted(get_lang(lang_name).fixers.keys())
   456→        except (ImportError, ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError):
   457→            fixer_names = []
   458→        fixer_list = ", ".join(fixer_names) if fixer_names else "none yet"
   459→        fixer_help_lines.append(f"fixers ({lang_name}): {fixer_list}")
   460→    fixer_help_lines.append("special: review — prepare structured review data")
   461→    return fixer_help_lines
   462→
   463→
   464→def _add_fix_parser(sub, langs: list[str]) -> None:
   465→    p_fix = sub.add_parser(
   466→        "fix",
   467→        help="Auto-fix mechanical issues",
   468→        epilog="\n".join(_fixer_help_lines(langs)),
   469→    )
   470→    p_fix.add_argument("fixer", type=str, help="What to fix")
   471→    p_fix.add_argument("--path", type=str, default=None)
   472→    p_fix.add_argument("--state", type=str, default=None)
   473→    p_fix.add_argument(
   474→        "--dry-run",
   475→        action="store_true",
   476→        help="Show what would change without modifying files",
   477→    )
```

```
    83→    """Normalize a set or list of paths to sorted relative paths (max 10)."""
    84→    if isinstance(s, set):

    86→    return [rel(x) for x in list(s)[:10]]

    93→    parsed = int(value)
    94→    return parsed if parsed > 0 else None
```

```
    17→    if not isinstance(focus, dict):
    18→        return ""
    19→
    20→    selected_raw = focus.get("selected_count", 0)
    21→    try:
    22→        selected_count = max(0, int(selected_raw))
    23→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    24→        selected_count = 0

    26→    issues = focus.get("issues", [])

    28→        issues = []
    29→
    30→    if selected_count <= 0 or not issues:
    31→        return ""
    32→
    33→    lines: list[str] = []
    34→    lines.append(
    35→        "Previously flagged issues (from past reviews of these dimensions):"

    53→        lines.append(line)
```

Perfect! Now I have a comprehensive understanding. Let me create a summary document based on the code I've reviewed:

## Summary: Codex Review Batch Timing and Configuration

Based on my analysis of the desloppify codebase, here are the key findings regarding batch execution timing:

### 1. **Batch Composition**

Each batch contains:
- **File Count**: Max 80 files per batch by default (`DEFAULT_REVIEW_BATCH_MAX_FILES = 80`)
  - This is configurable via `review_batch_max_files` in config, or `max_files_per_batch` parameter
  - 8 primary investigation batches (Architecture & Coupling, Conventions & Errors, Abstractions & Dependencies, Testing & API, Authorization, AI Debt & Migrations, Package Organization, Governance & Contracts)
  - 1 optional "Cross-cutting Sweep" fallback batch when dimensions don't map directly
  - 1 optional "Mechanical Concern Signals" batch from structural pattern concerns
  
- **Dimensions per Batch**: Varies (typically 2-4 dimensions per batch)
  - Architecture & Coupling: 2 dimensions
  - Conventions & Errors: 3 dimensions
  - Abstractions & Dependencies: 4 dimensions
  - Testing & API: 3 dimensions
  - Authorization: 2 dimensions
  - AI Debt & Migrations: 3 dimensions
  - Package Organization: 2 dimensions
  - Governance & Contracts: 4 dimensions

- **Findings Cap**: Based on dimension count per batch
  - Max `findings_cap = max_batch_findings_for_dimension_count(len(dims))`
  - This scales with number of dimensions being evaluated

### 2. **Two Different Timeout Defaults**

**Critical discrepancy found:**

- **CLI Argument Default** (`parser_groups_admin.py:292-295`): `20 * 60 = 1200 seconds = 20 minutes`
  ```python
  "--batch-timeout-seconds",
  type=int,
  default=20 * 60,  # 1200 seconds
  help="Per-batch runner timeout in seconds (default: 1200)",
  ```

- **batches.py default** (for `do_run_batches` orchestration, line 340-343): `2 * 60 * 60 = 7200 seconds = 2 hours`
  ```python
  batch_timeout_seconds = _coerce_positive_int(
      getattr(args, "batch_timeout_seconds", None),
      default=2 * 60 * 60,  # 7200 seconds = 2 hours
      minimum=1,
  )
  ```

- **batch.py default** (for `_do_run_batches` which calls runner_helpers): `CODEX_BATCH_TIMEOUT_SECONDS = 20 * 60` (line 29)
  ```python
  CODEX_BATCH_TIMEOUT_SECONDS = 20 * 60  # 1200 seconds
  ```

**Why the discrepancy?** 
- `batch.py` is the CODEX runner (subprocess invocation of Claude/local agent)
- `batches.py` is the higher-level orchestration for parallel batch scheduling
- These represent different workflow paths in the review system

### 3. **CLI Flag Usage**

The `--batch-timeout-seconds` flag flows through:
1. `parser_groups_admin.py`: Defined with default of 20 minutes
2. `batch.py` (_do_run_batches): Uses `CODEX_BATCH_TIMEOUT_SECONDS` constant (20 min) as default, but overrides with CLI arg if provided
3. `batches.py` (do_run_batches): Uses 2-hour default if CLI arg not provided
4. `runner_helpers.py`: Passes timeout to `CodexBatchRunnerDeps.timeout_seconds`
5. Actual enforcement in subprocess runner (line 317-322, 410): Kills process after `deps.timeout_seconds` elapsed

### 4. **Expected Execution Duration**

The system calculates worst-case execution time at runtime:
```python
# From batches.py lines 398-411
waves = max(1, math.ceil(total_batches / max(1, effective_workers)))
worst_case_seconds = waves * batch_timeout_seconds
worst_case_minutes = max(1, math.ceil(worst_case_seconds / 60))

# Example: 8 batches, 3 workers, 20-min timeout per batch
# waves = ceil(8/3) = 3
# worst_case = 3 * 1200 = 3600 seconds = 60 minutes
```

### 5. **Stall Detection (Additional Safety Mechanism)**

Beyond the main timeout, there's a "stall kill" mechanism:
- `CODEX_BATCH_STALL_KILL_SECONDS = 120` seconds (line 30 in batch.py)
- CLI flag: `--batch-stall-kill-seconds` (default 120, can be 0 to disable)
- Behavior: Terminates batch if output file is stable AND runner streams are idle for this duration (lines 323-330 in runner_helpers.py)

### 6. **Recommended Timeout Decision**

**For a single batch execution:**

- **20 minutes (1200s)**: Good for small-to-medium codebases with 1-80 files per batch
  - CLI default (matches CODEX_BATCH_TIMEOUT_SECONDS)
  - Safe for typical review scenarios
  
- **45 minutes (2700s)**: Reasonable middle ground
  - Not explicitly coded, but could be useful for larger file sets or slower agents
  
- **2 hours (7200s)**: Used as orchestration-level default
  - Designed for complex, parallel runs with multiple waves of sequential batch groups
  - May be excessive for individual batch execution

**The 20-minute CLI default appears intentional** for the common case. The 2-hour default in `batches.py` is for orchestration timing—it's a worst-case upper bound for the entire multi-wave run, not per-batch.

### 7. **Key Files Referenced**

- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/batch.py` (CODEX runner, 20 min constant)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/batches.py` (orchestration, 2 hour default)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/cli_support/parser_groups_admin.py` (CLI flag, 20 min default)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/runner_helpers.py` (timeout enforcement)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/intelligence/review/prepare_batches.py` (batch definitions)
