    99→def run_stamp() -> str:
   100→    """Stable UTC run stamp for artifact paths."""
   101→    return datetime.now(UTC).strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
   102→
   103→
   104→def codex_batch_command(*, prompt: str, repo_root: Path, output_file: Path) -> list[str]:
   105→    """Build one codex exec command line for a batch prompt."""
   106→    effort = os.environ.get("DESLOPPIFY_CODEX_REASONING_EFFORT", "low").strip().lower()
   107→    if effort not in {"low", "medium", "high", "xhigh"}:
   108→        effort = "low"
   109→    return [
   110→        "codex",
   111→        "exec",
   112→        "--ephemeral",
   113→        "-C",
   114→        str(repo_root),
   115→        "-s",
   116→        "workspace-write",
   117→        "-c",
   118→        'approval_policy="never"',
   119→        "-c",
   120→        f'model_reasoning_effort="{effort}"',
   121→        "-o",
   122→        str(output_file),
   123→        prompt,
   124→    ]
   125→
   126→
   127→def run_codex_batch(
   128→    *,
   129→    prompt: str,
   130→    repo_root: Path,
   131→    output_file: Path,
   132→    log_file: Path,
   133→    deps: CodexBatchRunnerDeps,
   134→) -> int:
   135→    """Execute one codex batch and return a stable CLI-style status code."""
   136→    cmd = codex_batch_command(prompt=prompt, repo_root=repo_root, output_file=output_file)
   137→    retries_raw = deps.max_retries if isinstance(deps.max_retries, int) else 0
   138→    max_retries = max(0, retries_raw)
   139→    max_attempts = max_retries + 1
   140→    backoff_raw = (
   141→        float(deps.retry_backoff_seconds)
   142→        if isinstance(deps.retry_backoff_seconds, int | float)
   143→        else 0.0
   144→    )
   145→    retry_backoff_seconds = max(0.0, backoff_raw)
   146→    live_log_interval_seconds = (
   147→        float(deps.live_log_interval_seconds)
   148→        if isinstance(deps.live_log_interval_seconds, int | float)
   149→        and float(deps.live_log_interval_seconds) > 0
   150→        else 5.0
   151→    )
   152→    stall_after_output_seconds = (
   153→        int(deps.stall_after_output_seconds)
   154→        if isinstance(deps.stall_after_output_seconds, int | float)
   155→        and int(deps.stall_after_output_seconds) > 0
   156→        else 0
   157→    )
   158→    log_sections: list[str] = []
   159→
   160→    def _output_file_status_text() -> str:
   161→        if not output_file.exists():
   162→            return f"{output_file} (missing)"
   163→        try:
   164→            stat = output_file.stat()
   165→        except OSError as exc:
   166→            return f"{output_file} (exists; stat failed: {exc})"
   167→        modified_at = datetime.fromtimestamp(stat.st_mtime, tz=UTC).isoformat(
   168→            timespec="seconds"
   169→        )
   170→        return (
   171→            f"{output_file} (exists; bytes={stat.st_size}; "
   172→            f"modified={modified_at})"
   173→        )
   174→
   175→    def _output_file_has_json_payload() -> bool:
   176→        if not output_file.exists():
   177→            return False
   178→        try:
   179→            payload = json.loads(output_file.read_text())
   180→        except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError):
   181→            return False
   182→        return isinstance(payload, dict)
   183→
   184→    def _terminate_process(process: subprocess.Popen[str]) -> None:
   185→        if process.poll() is not None:
   186→            return
   187→        try:
   188→            process.terminate()
   189→            process.wait(timeout=3)
   190→            return
   191→        except Exception:
   192→            pass
   193→        try:
   194→            process.kill()
   195→            process.wait(timeout=3)
   196→        except Exception:
   197→            return
   198→
   199→    for attempt in range(1, max_attempts + 1):
   200→        header = f"ATTEMPT {attempt}/{max_attempts}\n$ {' '.join(cmd)}"
   201→        started_at_iso = datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   202→        started_monotonic = time.monotonic()
   203→        stdout_chunks: list[str] = []
   204→        stderr_chunks: list[str] = []
   205→        state_lock = threading.Lock()
   206→        stop_live_writer = threading.Event()
   207→        runner_note: list[str] = []
   208→        last_stream_activity = [started_monotonic]
   209→
   210→        def _write_live_snapshot() -> None:
   211→            elapsed_seconds = int(max(0.0, time.monotonic() - started_monotonic))
   212→            with state_lock:
   213→                stdout_preview = "".join(stdout_chunks)
   214→                stderr_preview = "".join(stderr_chunks)
   215→                note = runner_note[0] if runner_note else ""
   216→            note_block = f"\nRUNNER NOTE: {note}" if note else ""
   217→            deps.safe_write_text_fn(
   218→                log_file,
   219→                "\n\n".join(
   220→                    log_sections
   221→                    + [
   222→                        (
   223→                            f"{header}\n\n"
   224→                            "STATUS: running\n"
   225→                            f"STARTED AT: {started_at_iso}\n"
   226→                            f"ELAPSED: {elapsed_seconds}s\n"
   227→                            f"OUTPUT FILE: {_output_file_status_text()}"
   228→                            f"{note_block}\n\n"
   229→                            f"STDOUT (live):\n{stdout_preview}\n\n"
   230→                            f"STDERR (live):\n{stderr_preview}\n"
   231→                        )
   232→                    ]
   233→                ),
   234→            )
   235→
   236→        def _start_live_writer() -> threading.Thread:
   237→            def _loop() -> None:
   238→                while not stop_live_writer.wait(live_log_interval_seconds):
   239→                    _write_live_snapshot()
   240→
   241→            thread = threading.Thread(target=_loop, daemon=True)
   242→            thread.start()
   243→            return thread
   244→
   245→        _write_live_snapshot()
   246→
   247→        # Preferred path: Popen allows live stream capture + stall recovery.
   248→        # Compatibility path stays available for injected test runners.
   249→        use_popen_runner = bool(deps.use_popen_runner) and callable(
   250→            getattr(deps, "subprocess_popen", None)
   251→        )
   252→        if use_popen_runner:
   253→            writer_thread = _start_live_writer()
   254→            process: subprocess.Popen[str] | None = None
   255→            stdout_thread: threading.Thread | None = None
   256→            stderr_thread: threading.Thread | None = None
   257→            timed_out = False
   258→            recovered_from_stall = False
   259→            output_signature: tuple[int, int] | None = None
   260→            output_stable_since: float | None = None
   261→            try:
   262→                process = deps.subprocess_popen(
   263→                    cmd,
   264→                    stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
   265→                    stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
   266→                    text=True,
   267→                    bufsize=1,
   268→                )
   269→            except OSError as exc:
   270→                stop_live_writer.set()
   271→                writer_thread.join(timeout=2)
   272→                log_sections.append(f"{header}\n\nRUNNER ERROR:\n{exc}\n")
   273→                deps.safe_write_text_fn(log_file, "\n\n".join(log_sections))
   274→                return 127
   275→            except Exception as exc:  # pragma: no cover - defensive boundary
   276→                stop_live_writer.set()
   277→                writer_thread.join(timeout=2)
   278→                log_sections.append(f"{header}\n\nUNEXPECTED RUNNER ERROR:\n{exc}\n")
   279→                deps.safe_write_text_fn(log_file, "\n\n".join(log_sections))
   280→                return 1
   281→
   282→            def _drain_stream(stream, sink: list[str]) -> None:
   283→                if stream is None:
   284→                    return
   285→                try:
   286→                    for chunk in iter(stream.readline, ""):
   287→                        if not chunk:
   288→                            break
   289→                        with state_lock:
   290→                            sink.append(chunk)
   291→                            last_stream_activity[0] = time.monotonic()
   292→                except Exception as exc:  # pragma: no cover - defensive boundary
   293→                    with state_lock:
   294→                        sink.append(f"\n[stream read error: {exc}]\n")
   295→                finally:
   296→                    try:
   297→                        stream.close()
   298→                    except Exception:
   299→                        pass
   300→
   301→            stdout_thread = threading.Thread(
   302→                target=_drain_stream,
   303→                args=(process.stdout, stdout_chunks),
   304→                daemon=True,
   305→            )
   306→            stderr_thread = threading.Thread(
   307→                target=_drain_stream,
   308→                args=(process.stderr, stderr_chunks),
   309→                daemon=True,
   310→            )
   311→            stdout_thread.start()
   312→            stderr_thread.start()
   313→
   314→            while process.poll() is None:
   315→                now_monotonic = time.monotonic()
   316→                elapsed = int(max(0.0, now_monotonic - started_monotonic))
   317→                if elapsed >= deps.timeout_seconds:
   318→                    with state_lock:
   319→                        runner_note[:] = [f"timeout after {deps.timeout_seconds}s"]
   320→                    timed_out = True
   321→                    _terminate_process(process)
   322→                    break
   323→                if stall_after_output_seconds > 0:
   324→                    current_signature: tuple[int, int] | None = None
   325→                    try:
   326→                        stat = output_file.stat()
   327→                        current_signature = (int(stat.st_size), int(stat.st_mtime))
   328→                    except OSError:
   329→                        current_signature = None
   330→                    if current_signature is None:
   331→                        output_signature = None
   332→                        output_stable_since = None
   333→                    elif current_signature != output_signature:
   334→                        output_signature = current_signature
   335→                        output_stable_since = now_monotonic
   336→                    elif output_stable_since is not None:
   337→                        output_age = now_monotonic - output_stable_since
   338→                        stream_idle = now_monotonic - last_stream_activity[0]
   339→                        if (
   340→                            output_age >= stall_after_output_seconds
   341→                            and stream_idle >= stall_after_output_seconds
   342→                        ):
   343→                            with state_lock:
   344→                                runner_note[:] = [
   345→                                    (
   346→                                        "stall recovery triggered after "
   347→                                        f"{stall_after_output_seconds}s "
   348→                                        "with stable output file"
   349→                                    )
   350→                                ]
   351→                            recovered_from_stall = _output_file_has_json_payload()
   352→                            _terminate_process(process)
   353→                            break
   354→                deps.sleep_fn(min(live_log_interval_seconds, 1.0))
   355→
   356→            if process.poll() is None:
   357→                _terminate_process(process)
   358→            if stdout_thread is not None:
   359→                stdout_thread.join(timeout=2)
   360→            if stderr_thread is not None:
   361→                stderr_thread.join(timeout=2)
   362→            stop_live_writer.set()
   363→            writer_thread.join(timeout=2)
   364→            _write_live_snapshot()
   365→
   366→            stdout_text = "".join(stdout_chunks)
   367→            stderr_text = "".join(stderr_chunks)
   368→            if timed_out:
   369→                log_sections.append(
   370→                    f"{header}\n\nTIMEOUT after {deps.timeout_seconds}s\n\n"
   371→                    f"STDOUT:\n{stdout_text}\n\nSTDERR:\n{stderr_text}\n"
   372→                )
   373→                if _output_file_has_json_payload():
   374→                    log_sections.append(
   375→                        "Recovered timed-out batch from JSON output file; "
   376→                        "continuing as success."
   377→                    )
   378→                    deps.safe_write_text_fn(log_file, "\n\n".join(log_sections))
   379→                    return 0
   380→                deps.safe_write_text_fn(log_file, "\n\n".join(log_sections))
   381→                return 124
   382→            if recovered_from_stall:
   383→                log_sections.append(
   384→                    f"{header}\n\nSTALL RECOVERY after {stall_after_output_seconds}s "
   385→                    "of stable output and no stream activity.\n\n"
   386→                    f"STDOUT:\n{stdout_text}\n\nSTDERR:\n{stderr_text}\n"
   387→                )
   388→                if _output_file_has_json_payload():
   389→                    log_sections.append(
   390→                        "Recovered stalled batch from JSON output file; "
   391→                        "continuing as success."
   392→                    )
   393→                    deps.safe_write_text_fn(log_file, "\n\n".join(log_sections))
   394→                    return 0
   395→                deps.safe_write_text_fn(log_file, "\n\n".join(log_sections))
   396→                return 124
   397→
   398→            code = int(process.returncode or 0)
   399→            log_sections.append(
   400→                f"{header}\n\nSTDOUT:\n{stdout_text}\n\nSTDERR:\n{stderr_text}\n"
   401→            )
   402→        else:
   403→            # Compatibility path used by tests/injected runners.
   404→            writer_thread = _start_live_writer()
   405→            try:
   406→                result = deps.subprocess_run(
   407→                    cmd,
   408→                    capture_output=True,
   409→                    text=True,
   410→                    timeout=deps.timeout_seconds,
   411→                )
   412→            except deps.timeout_error as exc:
   413→                stop_live_writer.set()
   414→                writer_thread.join(timeout=2)
   415→                log_sections.append(
   416→                    f"{header}\n\nTIMEOUT after {deps.timeout_seconds}s\n{exc}\n"
   417→                )
   418→                deps.safe_write_text_fn(log_file, "\n\n".join(log_sections))
   419→                return 124
   420→            except OSError as exc:
   421→                stop_live_writer.set()
   422→                writer_thread.join(timeout=2)
   423→                log_sections.append(f"{header}\n\nRUNNER ERROR:\n{exc}\n")
   424→                deps.safe_write_text_fn(log_file, "\n\n".join(log_sections))
   425→                return 127
   426→            except (RuntimeError, ValueError) as exc:  # pragma: no cover - defensive boundary
   427→                stop_live_writer.set()
   428→                writer_thread.join(timeout=2)
   429→                log_sections.append(f"{header}\n\nUNEXPECTED RUNNER ERROR:\n{exc}\n")
   430→                deps.safe_write_text_fn(log_file, "\n\n".join(log_sections))
   431→                return 1
   432→            finally:
   433→                stop_live_writer.set()
   434→                writer_thread.join(timeout=2)
   435→
   436→            stdout_text = result.stdout or ""
   437→            stderr_text = result.stderr or ""
   438→            log_sections.append(
   439→                f"{header}\n\nSTDOUT:\n{stdout_text}\n\nSTDERR:\n{stderr_text}\n"
   440→            )
   441→            code = int(result.returncode)
   442→
   443→        if code == 0:
   444→            deps.safe_write_text_fn(log_file, "\n\n".join(log_sections))
   445→            return 0
   446→
   447→        combined = f"{stdout_text}\n{stderr_text}".lower()
   448→        is_transient = any(needle in combined for needle in _TRANSIENT_RUNNER_PHRASES)
   449→        if not is_transient or attempt >= max_attempts:
   450→            deps.safe_write_text_fn(log_file, "\n\n".join(log_sections))
   451→            return code
   452→
   453→        delay_seconds = retry_backoff_seconds * (2 ** (attempt - 1))
   454→        log_sections.append(
   455→            "Transient runner failure detected; "
   456→            f"retrying in {delay_seconds:.1f}s (attempt {attempt + 1}/{max_attempts})."
   457→        )
   458→        try:
   459→            if delay_seconds > 0:
   460→                deps.sleep_fn(delay_seconds)
   461→        except Exception:  # pragma: no cover - defensive boundary
   462→            # Retry should proceed even if delay hook fails.
   463→            pass
   464→
   465→    deps.safe_write_text_fn(log_file, "\n\n".join(log_sections))
   466→    return 1
   467→
   468→
   469→def run_followup_scan(
   470→    *,
   471→    lang_name: str,
   472→    scan_path: str,
   473→    deps: FollowupScanDeps,
   474→) -> int:
   475→    """Run a follow-up scan and return a non-zero status when it fails."""
   476→    scan_cmd = [
   477→        deps.python_executable,
   478→        "-m",
   479→        "desloppify",
   480→        "--lang",
   481→        lang_name,
   482→        "scan",
   483→        "--path",
   484→        scan_path,
   485→    ]
   486→    print(deps.colorize_fn("\n  Running follow-up scan...", "bold"))
   487→    try:
   488→        result = deps.subprocess_run(
   489→            scan_cmd,
   490→            cwd=str(deps.project_root),
   491→            timeout=deps.timeout_seconds,
   492→        )
   493→    except deps.timeout_error:
   494→        print(
   495→            deps.colorize_fn(
   496→                f"  Follow-up scan timed out after {deps.timeout_seconds}s.",
   497→                "yellow",
   498→            ),
   499→            file=sys.stderr,
   500→        )
   501→        return 124
   502→    except OSError as exc:
   503→        print(
   504→            deps.colorize_fn(f"  Follow-up scan failed: {exc}", "red"),
   505→            file=sys.stderr,
   506→        )
   507→        return 1
   508→    return int(getattr(result, "returncode", 0) or 0)
   509→
   510→
   511→def write_packet_snapshot(
   512→    packet: dict,
   513→    *,
   514→    stamp: str,
   515→    review_packet_dir: Path,
   516→    blind_path: Path,
   517→    safe_write_text_fn,
   518→) -> tuple[Path, Path]:
   519→    """Persist immutable and blind packet snapshots for runner workflows."""
   520→    review_packet_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   521→    packet_path = review_packet_dir / f"holistic_packet_{stamp}.json"
   522→    safe_write_text_fn(packet_path, json.dumps(packet, indent=2) + "\n")
   523→    blind_packet = _build_blind_packet(packet)
   524→    safe_write_text_fn(blind_path, json.dumps(blind_packet, indent=2) + "\n")
   525→    return packet_path, blind_path
   526→
   527→
   528→def _build_blind_packet(packet: dict) -> dict:
   529→    """Return a blind-review packet with score anchoring metadata removed."""
   530→    blind = deepcopy(packet)
   531→    for key in _BLIND_PACKET_DROP_KEYS:
   532→        blind.pop(key, None)
   533→
   534→    config = blind.get("config")
   535→    if isinstance(config, dict):
   536→        sanitized = _sanitize_blind_config(config)
   537→        if sanitized:
   538→            blind["config"] = sanitized
   539→        else:
   540→            blind.pop("config", None)
   541→    return blind
   542→
   543→
   544→def build_blind_packet(packet: dict) -> dict:
   545→    """Public wrapper for blind packet sanitization."""
   546→    return _build_blind_packet(packet)
   547→
   548→
   549→def _sanitize_blind_config(config: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
   550→    """Drop score/target hints from config while preserving unrelated options."""
   551→    sanitized: dict[str, Any] = {}
   552→    for key, value in config.items():
   553→        lowered = key.strip().lower()
   554→        if not lowered:
   555→            continue
   556→        if lowered in _BLIND_CONFIG_SCORE_HINT_KEYS:
   557→            continue
   558→        if "target" in lowered:
   559→            continue
   560→        if lowered.endswith("_score"):
   561→            continue
   562→        sanitized[key] = value
   563→    return sanitized
   564→
   565→
   566→def sha256_file(path: Path) -> str | None:
   567→    """Compute sha256 hex digest for path contents (or None on read failure)."""
   568→    try:
   569→        data = path.read_bytes()
   570→    except OSError:
   571→        return None
   572→    return sha256(data).hexdigest()
   573→
   574→
   575→def build_batch_import_provenance(
   576→    *,
   577→    runner: str,
   578→    blind_packet_path: Path,
   579→    run_stamp: str,
   580→    batch_indexes: list[int],
   581→) -> dict[str, Any]:
   582→    """Build provenance payload used to trust assessment-bearing imports."""
   583→    packet_hash = sha256_file(blind_packet_path)
   584→    batch_indexes_1 = sorted({int(index) + 1 for index in batch_indexes})
   585→    return {
   586→        "kind": "blind_review_batch_import",
   587→        "blind": True,
   588→        "runner": runner,
   589→        "run_stamp": run_stamp,
   590→        "created_at": datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
   591→        "batch_count": len(batch_indexes_1),
   592→        "batch_indexes": batch_indexes_1,
   593→        "packet_path": str(blind_packet_path),
   594→        "packet_sha256": packet_hash,
   595→    }
   596→
   597→
   598→def selected_batch_indexes(
   599→    *,
   600→    raw_selection: str | None,
   601→    batch_count: int,
   602→    parse_fn,
   603→    colorize_fn,
   604→) -> list[int]:
   605→    """Validate selected batch indexes or exit with a CLI error."""
   606→    try:
   607→        selected = parse_fn(raw_selection, batch_count)
   608→    except ValueError as exc:
   609→        print(colorize_fn(f"  Error: {exc}", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   610→        sys.exit(2)
   611→    if selected:
   612→        return selected
   613→    print(colorize_fn("  Error: no batches selected", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   614→    sys.exit(2)
   615→
   616→
   617→def prepare_run_artifacts(
   618→    *,
   619→    stamp: str,
   620→    selected_indexes: list[int],
   621→    batches: list[dict],
   622→    packet_path: Path,
   623→    run_root: Path,
   624→    repo_root: Path,
   625→    build_prompt_fn,
   626→    safe_write_text_fn,
   627→    colorize_fn,
   628→) -> tuple[Path, Path, dict[int, Path], dict[int, Path], dict[int, Path]]:
   629→    """Build prompt/output/log paths and persist prompts for selected batches."""
   630→    run_dir = run_root / stamp
   631→    prompts_dir = run_dir / "prompts"
   632→    results_dir = run_dir / "results"
   633→    logs_dir = run_dir / "logs"
   634→    prompts_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   635→    results_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   636→    logs_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   637→
   638→    selected_1_based = [idx + 1 for idx in selected_indexes]
   639→    print(colorize_fn(f"\n  Running holistic batches: {selected_1_based}", "bold"))
   640→    print(colorize_fn(f"  Run artifacts: {run_dir}", "dim"))
   641→
   642→    prompt_files: dict[int, Path] = {}
   643→    output_files: dict[int, Path] = {}
   644→    log_files: dict[int, Path] = {}
   645→    for idx in selected_indexes:
   646→        batch = batches[idx] if isinstance(batches[idx], dict) else {}
   647→        prompt_text = build_prompt_fn(
   648→            repo_root=repo_root,
   649→            packet_path=packet_path,
   650→            batch_index=idx,
   651→            batch=batch,
   652→        )
   653→        prompt_file = prompts_dir / f"batch-{idx + 1}.md"
   654→        output_file = results_dir / f"batch-{idx + 1}.raw.txt"
   655→        log_file = logs_dir / f"batch-{idx + 1}.log"
   656→        safe_write_text_fn(prompt_file, prompt_text)
   657→        prompt_files[idx] = prompt_file
   658→        output_files[idx] = output_file
   659→        log_files[idx] = log_file
   660→    return run_dir, logs_dir, prompt_files, output_files, log_files
   661→
   662→
   663→def execute_batches(
   664→    *,
   665→    selected_indexes: list[int],
   666→    prompt_files: dict[int, Path],
   667→    output_files: dict[int, Path],
   668→    log_files: dict[int, Path],
   669→    run_parallel: bool,
   670→    run_batch_fn,
   671→    safe_write_text_fn,
   672→    progress_fn=None,
   673→    max_parallel_workers: int | None = None,
   674→    heartbeat_seconds: float | None = 15.0,
   675→    clock_fn=time.monotonic,
   676→) -> list[int]:
   677→    """Execute batch prompts and return failed index list."""
   678→
   679→    def _emit_progress(
   680→        batch_index: int,
   681→        event: str,
   682→        code: int | None = None,
   683→        *,
   684→        details: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
   685→    ) -> None:
   686→        if not callable(progress_fn):
   687→            return
   688→        payload = details or {}
   689→        try:
   690→            progress_fn(batch_index, event, code, **payload)
   691→        except TypeError:
   692→            try:
   693→                progress_fn(batch_index, event, code)
   694→            except Exception:
   695→                return
   696→        except Exception:
   697→            return
   698→
   699→    failures: list[int] = []
   700→    if run_parallel:
   701→        requested_workers = (
   702→            int(max_parallel_workers)
   703→            if isinstance(max_parallel_workers, int) and max_parallel_workers > 0
   704→            else 8
   705→        )
   706→        max_workers = max(1, min(len(selected_indexes), requested_workers))
   707→        heartbeat = (
   708→            float(heartbeat_seconds)
   709→            if isinstance(heartbeat_seconds, int | float) and heartbeat_seconds > 0
   710→            else None
   711→        )
   712→        started_at: dict[int, float] = {}
   713→        started_at_lock = threading.Lock()
   714→        with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=max_workers) as executor:
   715→            futures = {}
   716→            for idx in selected_indexes:
   717→                _emit_progress(
   718→                    idx,
   719→                    "queued",
   720→                    None,
   721→                    details={"max_workers": max_workers},
   722→                )
   723→
   724→                def _run_one(batch_index: int) -> int:
   725→                    with started_at_lock:
   726→                        started_at[batch_index] = float(clock_fn())
   727→                    _emit_progress(
   728→                        batch_index,
   729→                        "start",
   730→                        None,
   731→                        details={"max_workers": max_workers},
   732→                    )
   733→                    return run_batch_fn(
   734→                        prompt=prompt_files[batch_index].read_text(),
   735→                        output_file=output_files[batch_index],
   736→                        log_file=log_files[batch_index],
   737→                    )
   738→
   739→                future = executor.submit(_run_one, idx)
   740→                futures[future] = idx
   741→
   742→            pending = set(futures.keys())
   743→
   744→            def _handle_completed_future(future) -> None:
   745→                idx = futures[future]
   746→                with started_at_lock:
   747→                    started_at_value = started_at.get(idx, float(clock_fn()))
   748→                try:
   749→                    code = future.result()
   750→                except Exception as exc:  # future.result() can raise any exception from the batch runner
   751→                    safe_write_text_fn(log_files[idx], f"Runner exception:\n{exc}\n")
   752→                    failures.append(idx)
   753→                    _emit_progress(
   754→                        idx,
   755→                        "done",
   756→                        1,
   757→                        details={"elapsed_seconds": int(max(0.0, clock_fn() - started_at_value))},
   758→                    )
   759→                    return
   760→                if code != 0:
   761→                    failures.append(idx)
   762→                _emit_progress(
   763→                    idx,
   764→                    "done",
   765→                    code,
   766→                    details={"elapsed_seconds": int(max(0.0, clock_fn() - started_at_value))},
   767→                )
   768→
   769→            if heartbeat is None:
   770→                for future in as_completed(pending):
   771→                    pending.discard(future)
   772→                    _handle_completed_future(future)
   773→                return failures
   774→
   775→            while pending:
   776→                try:
   777→                    future = next(as_completed(pending, timeout=heartbeat))
   778→                except FuturesTimeoutError:
   779→                    with started_at_lock:
   780→                        active_indexes = sorted(
   781→                            futures[fut] for fut in pending if futures[fut] in started_at
   782→                        )
   783→                    active_index_set = set(active_indexes)
   784→                    queued_indexes = sorted(
   785→                        futures[fut] for fut in pending if futures[fut] not in active_index_set
   786→                    )
   787→                    elapsed_seconds = {
   788→                        idx: int(
   789→                            max(
   790→                                0.0,
   791→                                clock_fn() - started_at.get(idx, clock_fn()),
   792→                            )
   793→                        )
   794→                        for idx in active_indexes
   795→                    }
   796→                    _emit_progress(
   797→                        -1,
   798→                        "heartbeat",
   799→                        None,
   800→                        details={
   801→                            "active_batches": active_indexes,
   802→                            "queued_batches": queued_indexes,
   803→                            "elapsed_seconds": elapsed_seconds,
   804→                            "active_count": len(active_indexes),
   805→                            "queued_count": len(queued_indexes),
   806→                            "total_count": len(selected_indexes),
   807→                        },
   808→                    )
   809→                    continue
   810→                pending.discard(future)
   811→                _handle_completed_future(future)
   812→        return failures
   813→
   814→    for idx in selected_indexes:
   815→        started_at = float(clock_fn())
   816→        _emit_progress(idx, "start", None, details={"max_workers": 1})
   817→        code = run_batch_fn(
   818→            prompt=prompt_files[idx].read_text(),
   819→            output_file=output_files[idx],
   820→            log_file=log_files[idx],
   821→        )
   822→        if code != 0:
   823→            failures.append(idx)
   824→        _emit_progress(
   825→            idx,
   826→            "done",
   827→            code,
   828→            details={"elapsed_seconds": int(max(0.0, clock_fn() - started_at))},
   829→        )
   830→    return failures
   831→
   832→
   833→def collect_batch_results(
   834→    *,
   835→    selected_indexes: list[int],
   836→    failures: list[int],
   837→    output_files: dict[int, Path],
   838→    allowed_dims: set[str],
   839→    extract_payload_fn,
   840→    normalize_result_fn,
   841→) -> tuple[list[BatchResult], list[int]]:
   842→    """Parse and normalize batch outputs, preserving prior failures."""
   843→    def _extract_payload_from_log(idx: int, raw_path: Path) -> dict[str, object] | None:
   844→        log_path = raw_path.parent.parent / "logs" / f"batch-{idx + 1}.log"
   845→        if not log_path.exists():
   846→            return None
   847→        try:
   848→            log_text = log_path.read_text()
   849→        except OSError:
   850→            return None
   851→
   852→        # Prefer the final STDOUT section for the attempt; if that fails, scan the full log.
   853→        stdout_marker = "\nSTDOUT:\n"
   854→        stderr_marker = "\n\nSTDERR:\n"
   855→        stdout_start = log_text.rfind(stdout_marker)
   856→        if stdout_start == -1 and log_text.startswith("STDOUT:\n"):
   857→            stdout_start = 0
   858→            stdout_offset = len("STDOUT:\n")
   859→        elif stdout_start >= 0:
   860→            stdout_offset = len(stdout_marker)
   861→        else:
   862→            stdout_offset = 0
   863→        if stdout_start >= 0:
   864→            start_idx = stdout_start + stdout_offset
   865→            stdout_end = log_text.find(stderr_marker, start_idx)
   866→            stdout_text = (
   867→                log_text[start_idx:] if stdout_end == -1 else log_text[start_idx:stdout_end]
   868→            )
   869→            payload = extract_payload_fn(stdout_text)
   870→            if payload is not None:
   871→                return payload
   872→            # If the batch log has a concrete STDOUT section but it contains no parseable
   873→            # payload, do not fallback to parsing the whole log. Full logs include the
   874→            # prompt template (often with JSON examples), which can generate misleading
   875→            # decode errors and hide the true runner failure in STDERR.
   876→            return None
   877→
   878→        return extract_payload_fn(log_text)
   879→
   880→    batch_results: list[BatchResult] = []
   881→    failure_set = set(failures)
   882→    for idx in selected_indexes:
   883→        had_execution_failure = idx in failure_set
   884→        raw_path = output_files[idx]
   885→        payload = None
   886→        parsed_from_log = False
   887→        if raw_path.exists():
   888→            try:
   889→                payload = extract_payload_fn(raw_path.read_text())
   890→            except OSError:
   891→                payload = None
   892→        if payload is None:
   893→            payload = _extract_payload_from_log(idx, raw_path)
   894→            parsed_from_log = payload is not None
   895→        if payload is None:
   896→            failure_set.add(idx)
   897→            continue
   898→        if parsed_from_log:
   899→            try:
   900→                raw_path.write_text(json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   901→            except OSError:
   902→                pass
   903→        try:
   904→            assessments, findings, dimension_notes, quality = normalize_result_fn(
   905→                payload,
   906→                allowed_dims,
   907→            )
   908→        except ValueError:
   909→            failure_set.add(idx)
   910→            continue
   911→        if had_execution_failure:
   912→            failure_set.discard(idx)
   913→        batch_results.append(
   914→            BatchResult(
   915→                batch_index=idx + 1,
   916→                assessments=assessments,
   917→                dimension_notes=dimension_notes,
   918→                findings=findings,
   919→                quality=quality,
   920→            )
   921→        )
   922→    return batch_results, sorted(failure_set)
   923→
   924→
   925→def _classify_runner_failure(log_text: str) -> str:
   926→    """Classify batch failure type from log contents."""
   927→    text = log_text.lower()
   928→    if "timeout after" in text:
   929→        return "timeout"
   930→    if any(phrase in text for phrase in _TRANSIENT_RUNNER_PHRASES):
   931→        return "stream_disconnect"
   932→    if (
   933→        "codex not found" in text
   934→        or ("no such file or directory" in text and "$ codex " in text)
   935→        or ("errno 2" in text and "codex" in text)
   936→    ):
   937→        return "runner_missing"
   938→    if any(
   939→        phrase in text
   940→        for phrase in (
   941→            "not authenticated",
   942→            "authentication failed",
   943→            "unauthorized",
   944→            "forbidden",
   945→            "login required",
   946→            "please login",
   947→            "access token",
   948→        )
   949→    ):
   950→        return "runner_auth"
   951→    if "runner exception" in text:
   952→        return "runner_exception"
   953→    return "unknown"
   954→
   955→
   956→def _has_codex_backend_connectivity_issue(log_text: str) -> bool:
   957→    """Return True when logs indicate Codex backend URL is unreachable."""
   958→    text = log_text.lower()
   959→    if "error sending request for url" not in text:
   960→        return False
   961→    return (
   962→        _CODEX_BACKEND_PATH_HINT in text
   963→        or _CODEX_BACKEND_HOST_HINT in text
   964→        or "nodename nor servname provided" in text
   965→        or "name or service not known" in text
   966→        or "temporary failure in name resolution" in text
   967→    )
   968→
   969→
   970→def _summarize_failure_categories(*, failures: list[int], logs_dir: Path) -> dict[str, int]:
   971→    """Return counts by failure category for failed batches."""
   972→    categories: dict[str, int] = {}
   973→    for idx in sorted(set(failures)):
   974→        log_file = logs_dir / f"batch-{idx + 1}.log"
   975→        if not log_file.exists():
   976→            category = "missing_log"
   977→        else:
   978→            try:
   979→                category = _classify_runner_failure(log_file.read_text())
   980→            except OSError:
   981→                category = "log_read_error"
   982→        categories[category] = categories.get(category, 0) + 1
   983→    return categories
   984→
   985→
   986→def _runner_failure_hints(*, failures: list[int], logs_dir: Path) -> list[str]:
   987→    """Infer common runner environment failures from batch logs."""
   988→    hints: list[str] = []
   989→    for idx in sorted(set(failures)):
   990→        log_file = logs_dir / f"batch-{idx + 1}.log"
   991→        try:
   992→            raw = log_file.read_text()
   993→        except OSError:
   994→            continue
   995→        text = raw.lower()
   996→        if (
   997→            "codex not found" in text
   998→            or ("no such file or directory" in text and "$ codex " in text)
   999→            or ("errno 2" in text and "codex" in text)
  1000→        ):
  1001→            hint = (
  1002→                "codex CLI not found on PATH. Install Codex CLI and verify `codex --version`."
  1003→            )
  1004→            if hint not in hints:
  1005→                hints.append(hint)
  1006→        if any(
  1007→            phrase in text
  1008→            for phrase in (
  1009→                "not authenticated",
  1010→                "authentication failed",
  1011→                "unauthorized",
  1012→                "forbidden",
  1013→                "login required",
  1014→                "please login",
  1015→                "access token",
  1016→            )
  1017→        ):
  1018→            hint = "codex runner appears unauthenticated. Run `codex login` and retry."
  1019→            if hint not in hints:
  1020→                hints.append(hint)
  1021→        if any(phrase in text for phrase in _TRANSIENT_RUNNER_PHRASES):
  1022→            hint = (
  1023→                "Transient Codex connectivity issue detected. Retry with "
  1024→                "`--batch-max-retries 2 --batch-retry-backoff-seconds 2` and, if needed, "
  1025→                "lower concurrency via `--max-parallel-batches 1`."
  1026→            )
  1027→            if hint not in hints:
  1028→                hints.append(hint)
  1029→        if _has_codex_backend_connectivity_issue(text):
  1030→            hint = (
  1031→                "Codex runner cannot reach chatgpt.com backend from this environment. "
  1032→                "Check outbound HTTPS/DNS/proxy access, or use cloud fallback: "
  1033→                "`desloppify review --external-start --external-runner claude`."
  1034→            )
  1035→            if hint not in hints:
  1036→                hints.append(hint)
  1037→        if "failed to load skill" in text and "missing yaml frontmatter" in text:
  1038→            hint = (
  1039→                "Codex loaded an invalid local skill file. Fix/remove malformed "
  1040→                "SKILL.md entries under `~/.codex/skills` to reduce runner noise."
  1041→            )
  1042→            if hint not in hints:
  1043→                hints.append(hint)
  1044→    return hints
  1045→
  1046→
  1047→def _print_failures_report(
  1048→    *,
  1049→    failures: list[int],
  1050→    packet_path: Path,
  1051→    logs_dir: Path,
  1052→    colorize_fn,
  1053→) -> None:
  1054→    """Render retry guidance for failed batches."""
  1055→    failed_1 = sorted({idx + 1 for idx in failures})
  1056→    failed_csv = ",".join(str(i) for i in failed_1)
  1057→    print(colorize_fn(f"\n  Failed batches: {failed_1}", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
  1058→    categories = _summarize_failure_categories(failures=failures, logs_dir=logs_dir)
  1059→    if categories:
  1060→        labels = {
  1061→            "timeout": "timeout",
  1062→            "stream_disconnect": "stream disconnect",
  1063→            "runner_missing": "runner missing",
  1064→            "runner_auth": "runner auth",
  1065→            "runner_exception": "runner exception",
  1066→            "missing_log": "missing log",
  1067→            "log_read_error": "log read error",
  1068→            "unknown": "unknown",
  1069→        }
  1070→        category_segments = [
  1071→            f"{labels.get(name, name)}={count}"
  1072→            for name, count in sorted(categories.items())
  1073→        ]
  1074→        print(
  1075→            colorize_fn(
  1076→                f"  Failure categories: {', '.join(category_segments)}",
  1077→                "yellow",
  1078→            ),
  1079→            file=sys.stderr,
  1080→        )
  1081→        if categories.get("timeout", 0) > 0:
  1082→            print(
  1083→                colorize_fn(
  1084→                    "  Timeout tuning: lower concurrency with `--max-parallel-batches 1..3` "
  1085→                    "or increase `--batch-timeout-seconds` for long-running reviews.",
  1086→                    "yellow",
  1087→                ),
  1088→                file=sys.stderr,
  1089→            )
  1090→        if categories.get("stream_disconnect", 0) > 0:
  1091→            print(
  1092→                colorize_fn(
  1093→                    "  Connectivity tuning: enable retries with `--batch-max-retries 2` "
  1094→                    "and `--batch-retry-backoff-seconds 2`, then retry failed batches.",
  1095→                    "yellow",
  1096→                ),
  1097→                file=sys.stderr,
  1098→            )
  1099→    print(colorize_fn("  Retry command:", "yellow"), file=sys.stderr)
  1100→    print(
  1101→        colorize_fn(
  1102→            f"    desloppify review --run-batches --packet {packet_path} --only-batches {failed_csv}",
  1103→            "yellow",
  1104→        ),
  1105→        file=sys.stderr,
  1106→    )
  1107→    for idx_1 in failed_1:
  1108→        log_file = logs_dir / f"batch-{idx_1}.log"
  1109→        print(colorize_fn(f"    log: {log_file}", "dim"), file=sys.stderr)
  1110→    hints = _runner_failure_hints(failures=failures, logs_dir=logs_dir)
  1111→    if hints:
  1112→        print(colorize_fn("  Environment hints:", "yellow"), file=sys.stderr)
  1113→        for hint in hints:
  1114→            print(colorize_fn(f"    {hint}", "dim"), file=sys.stderr)
  1115→
  1116→
  1117→def print_failures(
  1118→    *,
  1119→    failures: list[int],
  1120→    packet_path: Path,
  1121→    logs_dir: Path,
  1122→    colorize_fn,
  1123→) -> None:
  1124→    """Render retry guidance for failed batches without exiting."""
  1125→    _print_failures_report(
  1126→        failures=failures,
  1127→        packet_path=packet_path,
  1128→        logs_dir=logs_dir,
  1129→        colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
  1130→    )
  1131→
  1132→
  1133→def print_failures_and_exit(
  1134→    *,
  1135→    failures: list[int],
  1136→    packet_path: Path,
  1137→    logs_dir: Path,
  1138→    colorize_fn,
  1139→) -> None:
  1140→    """Render retry guidance for failed batches and exit non-zero."""
  1141→    _print_failures_report(
  1142→        failures=failures,
  1143→        packet_path=packet_path,
  1144→        logs_dir=logs_dir,
  1145→        colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
  1146→    )
  1147→    sys.exit(1)
  1148→

   610→class TestSiblingConventions:

   619→            return_value=[str(hooks / f"hook{i}.ts") for i in range(4)]

   622→        assert "hooks/" in ctx.sibling_conventions

   639→# ── File cache integration test ──────────────────────────────────
   640→
   641→
   642→class TestFileCache:
   643→    def test_build_context_uses_file_cache(self, mock_lang, empty_state, tmp_path):
   644→        """build_review_context should enable file cache for performance."""
   645→        f = tmp_path / "foo.ts"
   646→        f.write_text("function getData() {}\nclass Foo {}")
   647→        mock_lang.file_finder = MagicMock(return_value=[str(f)])
   648→
   649→        # Cache should be disabled before and after
   650→        assert not is_file_cache_enabled()
   651→        build_review_context(tmp_path, mock_lang, empty_state)
   652→        assert not is_file_cache_enabled()  # Cleaned up after
   653→
   654→    def test_build_context_reentrant_cache(self, mock_lang, empty_state, tmp_path):
   655→        """build_review_context shouldn't disable cache if caller already enabled it."""
   656→        f = tmp_path / "foo.ts"
   657→        f.write_text("function getData() {}\nclass Foo {}")
   658→        mock_lang.file_finder = MagicMock(return_value=[str(f)])
   659→
   660→        enable_file_cache()
   661→        try:
   662→            assert is_file_cache_enabled()
   663→            build_review_context(tmp_path, mock_lang, empty_state)
   664→            assert is_file_cache_enabled()  # Still enabled — didn't stomp caller
   665→        finally:
   666→            disable_file_cache()
   667→
   668→    def test_prepare_caches_across_phases(self, mock_lang, empty_state, tmp_path):
   669→        """prepare_review should enable cache for context + selection + extraction."""
   670→        f = tmp_path / "foo.ts"
   671→        f.write_text("export function getData() { return 42; }\n" * 25)
   672→        mock_lang.file_finder = MagicMock(return_value=[str(f)])
   673→
   674→        assert not is_file_cache_enabled()
   675→        prepare_review(tmp_path, mock_lang, empty_state)
   676→        assert not is_file_cache_enabled()  # Cleaned up after
   677→

   681→
   682→class TestHeadlineBugFix:
   683→    def test_headline_no_typeerror_when_headline_none_with_review_suffix(self):
   684→        """Regression: None + review_suffix shouldn't TypeError."""
   685→        # Force: no security prefix, headline_inner returns None, review_suffix non-empty
   686→        # stagnation + review findings + conditions that make headline_inner return None
   687→        result = _compute_headline(
   688→            "stagnation",
   689→            {},
   690→            {},
   691→            None,
   692→            None,
   693→            None,
   694→            None,  # obj_strict=None → headline_inner falls through to None
   695→            {"open": 0},
   696→            [],
   697→            open_by_detector={"review": 5},
   698→        )
   699→        # Should not crash — may return None or a string with review suffix
   700→        if result is not None:
   701→            assert isinstance(result, str)
   702→
   703→    def test_headline_review_only_no_security_no_inner(self):
   704→        """When only review_suffix exists, returns it cleanly."""
   705→        result = _compute_headline(
   706→            "stagnation",
   707→            {},
   708→            {},
   709→            None,
   710→            None,
   711→            None,
   712→            None,

   715→            open_by_detector={"review": 3},

   717→        if result is not None:

   719→            assert "3" in result
