```
   366→
   367→def _canonical_external_payload(
   368→    raw_payload: dict[str, Any],
   369→    *,
   370→    session: dict[str, Any],
   371→) -> dict[str, Any]:
   372→    """Return import payload with canonical provenance and required session token."""
   373→    session_meta = raw_payload.get("session")
   374→    if not isinstance(session_meta, dict):
   375→        print(
   376→            colorize(
   377→                "  Error: external reviewer payload must include top-level `session` object.",
   378→                "red",
   379→            ),
   380→            file=sys.stderr,
   381→        )
   382→        print(
   383→            colorize(
   384→                "  Expected: {\"session\":{\"id\":\"...\",\"token\":\"...\"},\"assessments\":{...},\"findings\":[...]}",
   385→                "dim",
   386→            ),
   387→            file=sys.stderr,
   388→        )
   389→        sys.exit(1)
   390→
   391→    payload_id = str(session_meta.get("id", "")).strip()
   392→    payload_token = str(session_meta.get("token", "")).strip()
   393→    expected_id = str(session.get("session_id", "")).strip()
   394→    expected_token = str(session.get("token", "")).strip()
   395→    if payload_id != expected_id or payload_token != expected_token:
   396→        print(
   397→            colorize(
   398→                "  Error: session id/token mismatch in external reviewer payload.",
   399→                "red",
   400→            ),
   401→            file=sys.stderr,
   402→        )
   403→        print(
   404→            colorize("  Regenerate output using the session template/instructions.", "yellow"),
   405→            file=sys.stderr,
   406→        )
   407→        sys.exit(1)
   408→
   409→    payload = dict(raw_payload)
   410→    payload.pop("session", None)
   411→    payload.pop("provenance", None)
   412→    payload["provenance"] = {
   413→        "kind": "blind_review_batch_import",
   414→        "blind": True,
   415→        "runner": str(session.get("runner", "claude")),
   416→        "run_stamp": str(session.get("session_id", "")),
   417→        "created_at": _iso_seconds(_utc_now()),
   418→        "packet_path": str(session.get("blind_packet_path", "")),
   419→        "packet_sha256": str(session.get("packet_sha256", "")),
   420→        "external_session_id": expected_id,
   421→    }
   422→    return payload
   423→
   424→
   425→def _ensure_session_open_or_exit(session: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   426→    status = str(session.get("status", "open")).strip().lower()
   427→    if status == "open":
   428→        return
   429→    print(
   430→        colorize(
   431→            f"  Error: session is not open (status={status or 'unknown'}). Start a new session with --external-start.",
   432→            "red",
   433→        ),
   434→        file=sys.stderr,
   435→    )
   436→    sys.exit(1)
   437→
   438→
   439→def _ensure_session_not_expired_or_exit(session: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   440→    expires_at = _parse_iso(session.get("expires_at"))
   441→    if expires_at is None:
   442→        print(
   443→            colorize(
   444→                "  Error: session metadata is missing/invalid expires_at.",
   445→                "red",
   446→            ),
   447→            file=sys.stderr,
   448→        )
   449→        sys.exit(1)
   450→    now = _utc_now()
   451→    if now <= expires_at:
   452→        return
   453→    print(
   454→        colorize(
   455→            f"  Error: session expired at {session.get('expires_at')}. Start a new session with --external-start.",
   456→            "red",
   457→        ),
   458→        file=sys.stderr,
   459→    )
   460→    sys.exit(1)
   461→
   462→
   463→def do_external_submit(
   464→    *,
   465→    import_file: str,
   466→    session_id: str,
   467→    state: dict,
   468→    lang,
   469→    state_file,
   470→    config: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
   471→    allow_partial: bool = False,
   472→    scan_after_import: bool = False,
   473→    scan_path: str = ".",
   474→    dry_run: bool = False,
   475→) -> None:
   476→    """Submit external reviewer output via session, adding canonical provenance."""
   477→    config = config or {}
   478→    session_path, session = _session_payload_or_exit(session_id)
   479→    _ensure_session_open_or_exit(session)
   480→    _ensure_session_not_expired_or_exit(session)
   481→
   482→    if str(session.get("runner", "")).strip().lower() not in _EXTERNAL_SUPPORTED_RUNNERS:
   483→        print(
   484→            colorize(
   485→                "  Error: only Claude external sessions currently support durable score submit.",
   486→                "red",
   487→            ),
   488→            file=sys.stderr,
   489→        )
   490→        sys.exit(1)
   491→
   492→    findings_path = Path(import_file)
   493→    raw_payload = _load_json_object(findings_path, label="external findings")
   494→    canonical_payload = _canonical_external_payload(raw_payload, session=session)
   495→
   496→    stamp = runner_helpers_mod.run_stamp()
   497→    session_dir = session_path.parent
   498→    canonical_path = session_dir / f"canonical_import_{stamp}.json"
   499→    safe_write_text(canonical_path, json.dumps(canonical_payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   500→
   501→    if dry_run:
   502→        do_validate_import(
   503→            str(canonical_path),
   504→            lang,
   505→            allow_partial=allow_partial,
   506→            attested_external=True,
   507→            manual_attest=str(session.get("attest", EXTERNAL_ATTEST_TEXT)),
   508→        )
   509→        return
   510→
   511→    do_import(
   512→        str(canonical_path),
   513→        state,
   514→        lang,
   515→        state_file,
   516→        config=config,
   517→        allow_partial=allow_partial,
   518→        attested_external=True,
   519→        manual_attest=str(session.get("attest", EXTERNAL_ATTEST_TEXT)),
   520→    )
   521→
   522→    submitted_at = _iso_seconds(_utc_now())
   523→    session["status"] = "submitted"
   524→    session["submitted_at"] = submitted_at
   525→    session["submitted_input_file"] = str(findings_path)
   526→    session["submitted_canonical_file"] = str(canonical_path)
   527→    safe_write_text(session_path, json.dumps(session, indent=2) + "\n")
   528→
   529→    if scan_after_import:
   530→        from .batch import FOLLOWUP_SCAN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS
   531→
   532→        code = runner_helpers_mod.run_followup_scan(
   533→            lang_name=lang.name,
   534→            scan_path=scan_path,
   535→            deps=runner_helpers_mod.FollowupScanDeps(
   536→                project_root=PROJECT_ROOT,
   537→                timeout_seconds=FOLLOWUP_SCAN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,
   538→                python_executable=sys.executable,
   539→                subprocess_run=subprocess.run,
   540→                timeout_error=subprocess.TimeoutExpired,
   541→                colorize_fn=colorize,
   542→            ),
   543→        )
   544→        if code != 0:
   545→            raise SystemExit(code)
   546→
   547→
   548→__all__ = ["do_external_start", "do_external_submit", "EXTERNAL_ATTEST_TEXT"]
   549→
```

```
1→"""Core batch processing helpers for holistic review workflows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→from typing import Any
     8→
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.review.batch_scoring import DimensionMergeScorer
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.review.batch_prompt_template import render_batch_prompt
    11→from desloppify.intelligence.review.feedback_contract import (
    12→    DIMENSION_NOTE_ISSUES_KEY,
    13→    HIGH_SCORE_ISSUES_NOTE_THRESHOLD,
    14→    LEGACY_DIMENSION_NOTE_ISSUES_KEY,
    15→    LEGACY_REVIEW_QUALITY_HIGH_SCORE_MISSING_ISSUES_KEY,
    16→    LOW_SCORE_FINDING_THRESHOLD,
    17→    REVIEW_QUALITY_HIGH_SCORE_MISSING_ISSUES_KEY,
    18→)
    19→
    20→_DIMENSION_SCORER = DimensionMergeScorer()
    21→
    22→
    23→def parse_batch_selection(raw: str | None, batch_count: int) -> list[int]:
    24→    """Parse optional 1-based CSV list of batches."""
    25→    if not raw:
    26→        return list(range(batch_count))
    27→
    28→    selected: list[int] = []
    29→    seen: set[int] = set()
    30→    for token in raw.split(","):
    31→        text = token.strip()
    32→        if not text:
    33→            continue
    34→        idx_1 = int(text)
    35→        if idx_1 < 1 or idx_1 > batch_count:
    36→            raise ValueError(f"batch index {idx_1} out of range 1..{batch_count}")
    37→        idx_0 = idx_1 - 1
    38→        if idx_0 in seen:
    39→            continue
    40→        seen.add(idx_0)
    41→        selected.append(idx_0)
    42→    return selected
    43→
    44→
    45→def extract_json_payload(raw: str, *, log_fn) -> dict[str, object] | None:
    46→    """Best-effort extraction of first JSON object from agent output text."""
    47→    text = raw.strip()
    48→    if not text:
    49→        return None
    50→
    51→    decoder = json.JSONDecoder()
    52→    last_decode_error: json.JSONDecodeError | None = None
    53→    for start, ch in enumerate(text):
    54→        if ch not in "{[":
    55→            continue
    56→        try:
    57→            obj, _ = decoder.raw_decode(text[start:])
    58→        except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
    59→            last_decode_error = exc
    60→            continue
    61→        if (
    62→            isinstance(obj, dict)
    63→            and isinstance(obj.get("assessments"), dict)
    64→            and isinstance(obj.get("findings"), list)
    65→        ):
    66→            return obj
    67→    if last_decode_error is not None:
    68→        log_fn(f"  batch output JSON parse failed: {last_decode_error.msg}")
    69→    else:
    70→        log_fn("  batch output JSON parse failed: no valid payload found")
    71→    return None
    72→
    73→
    74→def _validate_dimension_note(
    75→    key: str,
    76→    note_raw: object,
    77→) -> tuple[list[object], str, str, str, str]:
    78→    """Validate a single dimension_notes entry and return parsed fields.
    79→
    80→    Returns (evidence, impact_scope, fix_scope, confidence, issues_preventing_higher_score).
    81→    Raises ValueError on invalid structure.
    82→    """
    83→    if not isinstance(note_raw, dict):
    84→        raise ValueError(
    85→            f"dimension_notes missing object for assessed dimension: {key}"
    86→        )
    87→    evidence = note_raw.get("evidence")
    88→    impact_scope = note_raw.get("impact_scope")
    89→    fix_scope = note_raw.get("fix_scope")
    90→    if not isinstance(evidence, list) or not evidence:
    91→        raise ValueError(
    92→            f"dimension_notes.{key}.evidence must be a non-empty array"
    93→        )
    94→    if not isinstance(impact_scope, str) or not impact_scope.strip():
    95→        raise ValueError(
    96→            f"dimension_notes.{key}.impact_scope must be a non-empty string"
    97→        )
    98→    if not isinstance(fix_scope, str) or not fix_scope.strip():
    99→        raise ValueError(
   100→            f"dimension_notes.{key}.fix_scope must be a non-empty string"
   101→        )
   102→
   103→    confidence_raw = str(note_raw.get("confidence", "medium")).strip().lower()
   104→    confidence = (
   105→        confidence_raw if confidence_raw in {"high", "medium", "low"} else "medium"
   106→    )
   107→    issues_note = str(note_raw.get(DIMENSION_NOTE_ISSUES_KEY, "")).strip()
   108→    if not issues_note:
   109→        issues_note = str(note_raw.get(LEGACY_DIMENSION_NOTE_ISSUES_KEY, "")).strip()
   110→    return evidence, impact_scope, fix_scope, confidence, issues_note
   111→
   112→
   113→def _normalize_abstraction_sub_axes(
   114→    note_raw: dict[str, object],
   115→    abstraction_sub_axes: tuple[str, ...],
   116→) -> dict[str, float]:
   117→    """Extract and clamp abstraction_fitness sub-axis scores from a note."""
   118→    sub_axes_raw = note_raw.get("sub_axes")
   119→    if sub_axes_raw is not None and not isinstance(sub_axes_raw, dict):
   120→        raise ValueError(
   121→            "dimension_notes.abstraction_fitness.sub_axes must be an object"
   122→        )
   123→    if not isinstance(sub_axes_raw, dict):
   124→        return {}
   125→
   126→    normalized: dict[str, float] = {}
   127→    for axis in abstraction_sub_axes:
   128→        axis_value = sub_axes_raw.get(axis)
   129→        if axis_value is None:
   130→            continue
   131→        if isinstance(axis_value, bool) or not isinstance(
   132→            axis_value, int | float
   133→        ):
   134→            raise ValueError(
   135→                f"dimension_notes.abstraction_fitness.sub_axes.{axis} "
   136→                "must be numeric"
   137→            )
   138→        normalized[axis] = round(
   139→            max(0.0, min(100.0, float(axis_value))),
   140→            1,
   141→        )
   142→    return normalized
   143→
   144→
   145→def _normalize_findings(
   146→    raw_findings: object,
   147→    dimension_notes: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
   148→    *,
   149→    max_batch_findings: int,
   150→    low_score_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
```

```
    48→    try:

    55→def _coerce_positive_int(value: object, *, default: int, minimum: int = 1) -> int:
    56→    """Parse positive integer CLI/config inputs with safe defaults."""
    57→    if value is None:
    58→        return default
    59→    if not isinstance(value, int | float | str):
    60→        return default
    61→    try:
    62→        parsed = int(value)
    63→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    64→        return default
    65→    return parsed if parsed >= minimum else default

    77→        return default
    78→    return parsed if parsed >= 0.0 else default
```

```
    19→def _validate_runner(runner: str, *, colorize_fn) -> None:
    20→    """Validate review batch runner."""
    21→    if runner == "codex":
    22→        return
    23→    print(
    24→        colorize_fn(
    25→            f"  Error: unsupported runner '{runner}' (supported: codex)", "red"
    26→        ),

    32→def _require_batches(
    33→    packet: dict,
    34→    *,
    35→    colorize_fn,
    36→    suggested_prepare_cmd: str | None = None,
    37→) -> list[dict]:
    38→    """Return investigation batches or exit with a clear error."""
    39→    batches = packet.get("investigation_batches", [])
    40→    if isinstance(batches, list) and batches:
    41→        return batches
    42→    print(
    43→        colorize_fn("  Error: packet has no investigation_batches.", "red"),
    44→        file=sys.stderr,
    45→    )
    46→    if isinstance(suggested_prepare_cmd, str) and suggested_prepare_cmd.strip():
    47→        print(
    48→            colorize_fn(

    50→                "yellow",
    51→            ),
    52→            file=sys.stderr,
    53→        )
    54→    print(
    55→        colorize_fn(
    56→            "  Happy path: `desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import`.",
    57→            "dim",
    58→        ),
    59→        file=sys.stderr,
    60→    )
    61→    sys.exit(1)
    62→
    63→
    64→def _print_review_quality(quality: object, *, colorize_fn) -> None:
    65→    """Render merged review quality summary when present."""
    66→    if not isinstance(quality, dict):
    67→        return
    68→    coverage = quality.get("dimension_coverage")
    69→    density = quality.get("evidence_density")
    70→    high_missing_issue_note = quality.get(
    71→        REVIEW_QUALITY_HIGH_SCORE_MISSING_ISSUES_KEY
    72→    )
    73→    if not isinstance(high_missing_issue_note, int | float):
    74→        high_missing_issue_note = quality.get(
    75→            LEGACY_REVIEW_QUALITY_HIGH_SCORE_MISSING_ISSUES_KEY
    76→        )
    77→    finding_pressure = quality.get("finding_pressure")
    78→    dims_with_findings = quality.get("dimensions_with_findings")
    79→    if not isinstance(coverage, int | float) or not isinstance(density, int | float):
    80→        return
    81→
    82→    pressure_segment = ""
    83→    if isinstance(finding_pressure, int | float) and isinstance(dims_with_findings, int):
    84→        pressure_segment = (
    85→            f", finding-pressure {float(finding_pressure):.2f} "
    86→            f"across {dims_with_findings} dims"
    87→        )
    88→    print(
    89→        colorize_fn(
    90→            "  Review quality: "
    91→            f"dimension coverage {float(coverage):.2f}, "
    92→            f"evidence density {float(density):.2f}, "
    93→            f"high-score-missing-issue-note {int(high_missing_issue_note or 0)}"
    94→            f"{pressure_segment}",
    95→            "dim",
    96→        )
    97→    )

   100→def _collect_reviewed_files_from_batches(
   101→    *,
   102→    batches: list[dict[str, object]],
   103→    selected_indexes: list[int],
   104→) -> list[str]:
   105→    """Collect normalized file paths reviewed in the selected batch set."""
   106→    reviewed: list[str] = []
   107→    seen: set[str] = set()
   108→    for idx in selected_indexes:
   109→        if idx < 0 or idx >= len(batches):
   110→            continue
   111→        batch = batches[idx]
   112→        files = batch.get("files_to_read", [])
   113→        if not isinstance(files, list):
   114→            continue
   115→        for raw in files:
   116→            if not isinstance(raw, str):
   117→                continue
   118→            path = raw.strip().strip(",'\"")
   119→            if not path or path in {".", ".."}:
   120→                continue
   121→            if path.endswith("/"):
   122→                continue
   123→            if path in seen:
   124→                continue
   125→            seen.add(path)
   126→            reviewed.append(path)
   127→    return reviewed
   128→

   134→    if not isinstance(value, int | float | str):
   135→        return default
   136→    try:
   137→        parsed = int(value)
   138→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   139→        return default
   140→    return parsed if parsed >= minimum else default
```

```
   156→def _coerce_non_negative_float(value: object, *, default: float) -> float:
   157→    """Parse non-negative float CLI/config inputs with a safe default."""
   158→    if value is None:
   159→        return default
   160→    if not isinstance(value, int | float | str):
   161→        return default
   162→    try:
   163→        parsed = float(value)
   164→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   165→        return default
   166→    return parsed if parsed >= 0.0 else default

   169→def _normalize_dimension_list(raw: object) -> list[str]:
   170→    """Normalize dimension collections to a stable, de-duplicated list."""
   171→    if not isinstance(raw, list):
   172→        return []
   173→    out: list[str] = []
   174→    seen: set[str] = set()
   175→    for item in raw:
   176→        if not isinstance(item, str):
   177→            continue
   178→        dim = item.strip()
   179→        if not dim or dim in seen:
   180→            continue
   181→        seen.add(dim)
   182→        out.append(dim)
   183→    return out

   192→    return _normalize_dimension_list(default_dims)
```

## Summary: How `runner_helpers.py` Is Used in Desloppify

Based on a thorough analysis of the codebase, here's the complete contract boundary documentation for `runner_helpers.py`:

### 1. ALL IMPORTS OF runner_helpers (5 files)

- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/batch.py` — main orchestrator
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/batches.py` — execution flow helpers (does NOT import directly but receives injected functions)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/external.py` — external review sessions
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/tests/review/test_review_commands.py` — unit tests
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/tests/commands/test_direct_coverage_modules.py` — coverage tests

### 2. PUBLIC API (Lines 1150-1168 in runner_helpers.py)

**Exported types and functions:**
```
- BatchResult (dataclass)
- CodexBatchRunnerDeps (dataclass)
- FollowupScanDeps (dataclass)
- build_batch_import_provenance(runner, blind_packet_path, run_stamp, batch_indexes) → dict
- build_blind_packet(packet) → dict

- codex_batch_command(prompt, repo_root, output_file) → list[str]
- collect_batch_results(selected_indexes, failures, output_files, allowed_dims, extract_payload_fn, normalize_result_fn) → tuple[list[BatchResult], list[int]]
- execute_batches(selected_indexes, prompt_files, output_files, log_files, run_parallel, run_batch_fn, safe_write_text_fn, progress_fn, max_parallel_workers, heartbeat_seconds, clock_fn) → list[int]
- prepare_run_artifacts(stamp, selected_indexes, batches, packet_path, run_root, repo_root, build_prompt_fn, safe_write_text_fn, colorize_fn) → tuple[Path, Path, dict[int, Path], dict[int, Path], dict[int, Path]]
- print_failures(failures, packet_path, logs_dir, colorize_fn) → None
- print_failures_and_exit(failures, packet_path, logs_dir, colorize_fn) → None

```

### 3. CodexBatchRunnerDeps CONSTRUCTION (batch.py, lines 276-287)

**In production code (`batch.py`, `_do_run_batches()`):**
```python
runner_helpers_mod.CodexBatchRunnerDeps(
    timeout_seconds=batch_timeout_seconds,           # CLI arg: default 1200s (20min)
    subprocess_run=subprocess.run,                   # Real subprocess runner
    timeout_error=subprocess.TimeoutExpired,        # Real timeout exception
    safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,            # File writer utility
    use_popen_runner=(getattr(subprocess.run, "__module__", "") == "subprocess"),
    subprocess_popen=subprocess.Popen,             # Real Popen for live streaming
    live_log_interval_seconds=batch_live_log_interval_seconds,  # Derived from heartbeat
    stall_after_output_seconds=batch_stall_kill_seconds,        # CLI arg: default 120s
    max_retries=batch_max_retries,                 # CLI arg: default 1
    retry_backoff_seconds=batch_retry_backoff_seconds,  # CLI arg: default 2.0
)
```

**In tests:** Uses mocked `subprocess.run`, `subprocess.TimeoutExpired`, and custom file writers.

### 4. run_codex_batch USAGE (batch.py, line 271)

**How it's wrapped:**
```python
run_codex_batch_fn=lambda *, prompt, repo_root, output_file, log_file: 
    runner_helpers_mod.run_codex_batch(
        prompt=prompt,
        repo_root=repo_root,
        output_file=output_file,
        log_file=log_file,
        deps=<CodexBatchRunnerDeps>
    ),
```

**Called by:** `execute_batches()` via the `run_batch_fn` closure, which reads prompt files and manages the queue.

**Returns:** CLI exit code (0=success, 124=timeout, 127=runner missing, 1=other error)

### 5. collect_batch_results USAGE (batch.py, lines 239-254)

**Two critical callback parameters:**

**extract_payload_fn:**
```python
lambda raw: batch_core_mod.extract_json_payload(raw, log_fn=log)
```
- Extracts the first valid JSON object with structure: `{assessments: dict, findings: list}`
- Called for both the output file AND the batch log file (fallback)

**normalize_result_fn:**
```python
lambda payload, dims: batch_core_mod.normalize_batch_result(
    payload,
    dims,
    max_batch_findings=max_batch_findings_for_dimension_count(len(dims)),
    abstraction_sub_axes=ABSTRACTION_SUB_AXES,
)
```
- Converts raw JSON payload into canonical form
- Returns tuple: `(assessments: dict, findings: list, dimension_notes: dict, quality: dict)`
- Validates schema and raises ValueError on invalid structure

**Flow:**
1. Reads output files (or falls back to batch logs if execution failed but JSON exists)
2. Extracts JSON via `extract_payload_fn`
3. Normalizes via `normalize_result_fn`
4. Returns `BatchResult` objects (with `.to_dict()` method for serialization)

### 6. execute_batches USAGE (batch.py, lines 289)

**Key parameters:**

- **selected_indexes**: 0-based batch indices
- **run_parallel**: boolean; when True, uses ThreadPoolExecutor with heartbeat progress
- **run_batch_fn**: closure that calls `run_codex_batch()` with kwarg `prompt=`, `output_file=`, `log_file=`
- **progress_fn**: optional callback for batch progress events
  - Events: `"queued"`, `"start"`, `"done"`, `"heartbeat"`
  - Signature: `progress_fn(batch_index, event, code, **details)`
  - Gracefully ignores exceptions if callback fails

**Returns:** List of failed batch 0-based indices

### 7. FULL INTEGRATION FLOW (batches.py → batch.py)

```
batch.py._do_run_batches()
  ├─ Load/prepare packet via _load_or_prepare_packet()
  ├─ Call do_run_batches(batches_mod) with injected functions:
  │   ├ run_stamp_fn=runner_helpers_mod.run_stamp
  │   ├ selected_batch_indexes_fn=lambda...runner_helpers_mod.selected_batch_indexes()
  │   ├ prepare_run_artifacts_fn=lambda...runner_helpers_mod.prepare_run_artifacts()
  │   │   └─ build_prompt_fn=batch_core_mod.build_batch_prompt  [injected]
  │   ├ run_codex_batch_fn=lambda...runner_helpers_mod.run_codex_batch()
  │   ├ execute_batches_fn=runner_helpers_mod.execute_batches
  │   ├ collect_batch_results_fn=lambda...runner_helpers_mod.collect_batch_results()
  │   │   ├─ extract_payload_fn=lambda...batch_core_mod.extract_json_payload()
  │   │   └─ normalize_result_fn=lambda...batch_core_mod.normalize_batch_result()
  │   ├ build_import_provenance_fn=runner_helpers_mod.build_batch_import_provenance
  │   └─ do_import_fn=_do_import  [external; imports merged findings]
  │
  └─ batches.do_run_batches():
      1. Prepare artifacts (prompts/logs/results directories)
      2. execute_batches_fn() → parallel/serial batch execution
      3. collect_batch_results_fn() → parse & normalize outputs
      4. merge_batch_results_fn() → cross-batch aggregation  [batch_core_mod]
      5. build_import_provenance_fn() → add metadata
      6. do_import_fn() → save findings to state
      7. [Optional] run_followup_scan_fn()
```

### 8. EXTERNAL.PY USAGE (external.py)

Uses runner_helpers for:
- `run_stamp()` — session ID generation (line 74, 177, 496)
- `write_packet_snapshot()` — snapshot preparation (line 179)
- `sha256_file()` — packet validation (line 261)
- `run_followup_scan()` with FollowupScanDeps (line 532)

**FollowupScanDeps construction (external.py, line 535):**
```python
runner_helpers_mod.FollowupScanDeps(
    project_root=PROJECT_ROOT,
    timeout_seconds=FOLLOWUP_SCAN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,  # 45 * 60
    python_executable=sys.executable,
    subprocess_run=subprocess.run,
    timeout_error=subprocess.TimeoutExpired,
    colorize_fn=colorize,
)
```

### 9. KEY CONTRACTS

**For refactoring the internals safely:**

1. **CodexBatchRunnerDeps fields** — All are used by `run_codex_batch()`:
   - `timeout_seconds`, `subprocess_run`, `timeout_error` required for both runners (subprocess.run and subprocess.Popen paths)
   - `use_popen_runner` controls branch (preferred for live streaming)
   - `safe_write_text_fn` for log snapshots and final output
   - `live_log_interval_seconds`, `stall_after_output_seconds`, `max_retries`, `retry_backoff_seconds` all actively used
   - `sleep_fn` for delay injection (default=time.sleep)

2. **BatchResult fields** — frozen dataclass used for:
   - `.to_dict()` serialization when merging
   - Direct field access: `batch_index`, `assessments`, `dimension_notes`, `findings`, `quality`

3. **Callback signatures are load-bearing:**
   - `extract_payload_fn(raw: str) → dict[str, object] | None`
   - `normalize_result_fn(payload: dict, allowed_dims: set[str]) → tuple[dict, list, dict, dict]`
   - `progress_fn(batch_index: int, event: str, code: int | None, **details)` — must silently ignore exception
   - `run_batch_fn(*, prompt: str, output_file: Path, log_file: Path) → int`
   - `build_prompt_fn(*, repo_root: Path, packet_path: Path, batch_index: int, batch: dict) → str`

4. **Return value semantics:**
   - `run_codex_batch()` returns shell-style exit codes (0, 1, 124=timeout, 127=missing)
   - `execute_batches()` returns list of 0-based failed indices
   - `collect_batch_results()` returns (list[BatchResult], list[int]) where int list is failures that remain after final parse attempt

This architecture uses a **dependency injection pattern** to separate concerns: `runner_helpers.py` provides orchestration primitives, while higher-level logic (`batches.py`) composes them with domain-specific callbacks.

### Key Testing Patterns & Fixtures

**Test Fixture Pattern - shared_review_fixtures.py:**
```python
@pytest.fixture

    return build_empty_state()

@pytest.fixture
```

**Mock Injection Pattern:**
All runner_helpers functions receive their dependencies as parameters:
- `subprocess_run` → mocked for sync tests
- `subprocess_popen` → mocked for Popen tests
- `timeout_error` → injection point for TimeoutError/custom exceptions
- `safe_write_text_fn` → injected for log verification
- `sleep_fn` → injected to speed up tests (skip real sleep)
- `progress_fn` → injected to track progress events
- `extract_payload_fn` / `normalize_result_fn` → injected parsing logic

**Critical Test Data Patterns:**

I need to refactor `desloppify/app/commands/review/runner_helpers.py` for clarity. The file is 1168 lines. The main problem is `run_codex_batch()` (lines 127-466, ~340 lines) which is a monolith with:

1. **Two inline code paths**: Popen runner (lines 252-401) and subprocess.run compatibility runner (lines 402-441) with duplicated timeout/error/cleanup patterns
2. **6 nested closures**: `_output_file_status_text`, `_output_file_has_json_payload`, `_terminate_process`, `_write_live_snapshot`, `_start_live_writer`, `_drain_stream` — all defined inside the function, capturing `output_file`, `log_file`, `deps`, `log_sections`, etc. via closure
3. **Mutable-list-as-ref hacks**: `runner_note: list[str] = []` used as `runner_note[:]` and `last_stream_activity = [started_monotonic]` used as `last_stream_activity[0]` — poor-man's mutable cells for closure capture
4. **Deep nesting**: The stall detection loop (lines 314-354) is 4-5 levels deep
5. **`collect_batch_results`** (lines 833-922) grew a 35-line nested function `_extract_payload_from_log` doing manual string slicing

**Constraints:**
- MUST preserve the public API exactly: `CodexBatchRunnerDeps` dataclass fields, `run_codex_batch` signature/return codes (0, 1, 124, 127), `BatchResult`, `collect_batch_results` signature
- MUST NOT break existing tests (4 tests for `run_codex_batch`, 2 for `collect_batch_results`, 5 for failure reporting, 1 for `execute_batches`)
- Tests inject mocked deps via `CodexBatchRunnerDeps` — the `use_popen_runner=False` path is used by most tests, `use_popen_runner=True` by the stall recovery test
- The dep injection pattern is load-bearing and should be preserved
- `__all__` exports must stay the same
- Keep it in one file — this isn't big enough to warrant splitting

**What I want:**
- Extract nested closures to module-level or class methods
- Unify the two execution paths where possible (shared setup/teardown, different middle)
- Replace mutable-list hacks with proper state objects
- Flatten the stall detection logic
- Move `_extract_payload_from_log` out of `collect_batch_results`
- Keep function signatures stable for callers

```
   103→
   104→def codex_batch_command(*, prompt: str, repo_root: Path, output_file: Path) -> list[str]:
   105→    """Build one codex exec command line for a batch prompt."""
   106→    effort = os.environ.get("DESLOPPIFY_CODEX_REASONING_EFFORT", "low").strip().lower()
   107→    if effort not in {"low", "medium", "high", "xhigh"}:
   108→        effort = "low"
   109→    return [
   110→        "codex",
   111→        "exec",
   112→        "--ephemeral",
   113→        "-C",
   114→        str(repo_root),
   115→        "-s",
   116→        "workspace-write",
   117→        "-c",
   118→        'approval_policy="never"',
   119→        "-c",
   120→        f'model_reasoning_effort="{effort}"',
   121→        "-o",
   122→        str(output_file),
   123→        prompt,
   124→    ]
   125→
   126→
   127→def run_codex_batch(
   128→    *,
   129→    prompt: str,
   130→    repo_root: Path,
   131→    output_file: Path,
   132→    log_file: Path,
   133→    deps: CodexBatchRunnerDeps,
   134→) -> int:
   135→    """Execute one codex batch and return a stable CLI-style status code."""
   136→    cmd = codex_batch_command(prompt=prompt, repo_root=repo_root, output_file=output_file)
   137→    retries_raw = deps.max_retries if isinstance(deps.max_retries, int) else 0
   138→    max_retries = max(0, retries_raw)
   139→    max_attempts = max_retries + 1
   140→    backoff_raw = (
   141→        float(deps.retry_backoff_seconds)
   142→        if isinstance(deps.retry_backoff_seconds, int | float)
   143→        else 0.0
   144→    )
   145→    retry_backoff_seconds = max(0.0, backoff_raw)
   146→    live_log_interval_seconds = (
   147→        float(deps.live_log_interval_seconds)
   148→        if isinstance(deps.live_log_interval_seconds, int | float)
   149→        and float(deps.live_log_interval_seconds) > 0
   150→        else 5.0
   151→    )
   152→    stall_after_output_seconds = (
   153→        int(deps.stall_after_output_seconds)
   154→        if isinstance(deps.stall_after_output_seconds, int | float)
   155→        and int(deps.stall_after_output_seconds) > 0
   156→        else 0
   157→    )
   158→    log_sections: list[str] = []
   159→
   160→    def _output_file_status_text() -> str:
   161→        if not output_file.exists():
   162→            return f"{output_file} (missing)"
   163→        try:
   164→            stat = output_file.stat()
   165→        except OSError as exc:
   166→            return f"{output_file} (exists; stat failed: {exc})"
   167→        modified_at = datetime.fromtimestamp(stat.st_mtime, tz=UTC).isoformat(
   168→            timespec="seconds"
   169→        )
   170→        return (
   171→            f"{output_file} (exists; bytes={stat.st_size}; "
   172→            f"modified={modified_at})"
   173→        )
   174→
   175→    def _output_file_has_json_payload() -> bool:
   176→        if not output_file.exists():
   177→            return False
   178→        try:
   179→            payload = json.loads(output_file.read_text())
   180→        except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError):
   181→            return False
   182→        return isinstance(payload, dict)
   183→
   184→    def _terminate_process(process: subprocess.Popen[str]) -> None:
   185→        if process.poll() is not None:
   186→            return
   187→        try:
   188→            process.terminate()
   189→            process.wait(timeout=3)
   190→            return
   191→        except Exception:
   192→            pass
   193→        try:
   194→            process.kill()
   195→            process.wait(timeout=3)
   196→        except Exception:
   197→            return
   198→
   199→    for attempt in range(1, max_attempts + 1):
   200→        header = f"ATTEMPT {attempt}/{max_attempts}\n$ {' '.join(cmd)}"
   201→        started_at_iso = datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   202→        started_monotonic = time.monotonic()
   203→        stdout_chunks: list[str] = []
   204→        stderr_chunks: list[str] = []
   205→        state_lock = threading.Lock()
   206→        stop_live_writer = threading.Event()
   207→        runner_note: list[str] = []
   208→        last_stream_activity = [started_monotonic]
   209→
   210→        def _write_live_snapshot() -> None:
   211→            elapsed_seconds = int(max(0.0, time.monotonic() - started_monotonic))
   212→            with state_lock:
   213→                stdout_preview = "".join(stdout_chunks)
   214→                stderr_preview = "".join(stderr_chunks)
   215→                note = runner_note[0] if runner_note else ""
   216→            note_block = f"\nRUNNER NOTE: {note}" if note else ""
   217→            deps.safe_write_text_fn(
   218→                log_file,
   219→                "\n\n".join(
   220→                    log_sections
   221→                    + [
   222→                        (
   223→                            f"{header}\n\n"
   224→                            "STATUS: running\n"
   225→                            f"STARTED AT: {started_at_iso}\n"
   226→                            f"ELAPSED: {elapsed_seconds}s\n"
   227→                            f"OUTPUT FILE: {_output_file_status_text()}"
   228→                            f"{note_block}\n\n"
   229→                            f"STDOUT (live):\n{stdout_preview}\n\n"
   230→                            f"STDERR (live):\n{stderr_preview}\n"
   231→                        )
   232→                    ]
   233→                ),
   234→            )
   235→
   236→        def _start_live_writer() -> threading.Thread:
   237→            def _loop() -> None:
   238→                while not stop_live_writer.wait(live_log_interval_seconds):
   239→                    _write_live_snapshot()
   240→
   241→            thread = threading.Thread(target=_loop, daemon=True)
   242→            thread.start()
   243→            return thread
   244→
   245→        _write_live_snapshot()
   246→
   247→        # Preferred path: Popen allows live stream capture + stall recovery.
   248→        # Compatibility path stays available for injected test runners.
   249→        use_popen_runner = bool(deps.use_popen_runner) and callable(
   250→            getattr(deps, "subprocess_popen", None)
   251→        )
   252→        if use_popen_runner:
   253→            writer_thread = _start_live_writer()
   254→            process: subprocess.Popen[str] | None = None
   255→            stdout_thread: threading.Thread | None = None
   256→            stderr_thread: threading.Thread | None = None
   257→            timed_out = False
   258→            recovered_from_stall = False
   259→            output_signature: tuple[int, int] | None = None
   260→            output_stable_since: float | None = None
   261→            try:
   262→                process = deps.subprocess_popen(
   263→                    cmd,
   264→                    stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
   265→                    stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
   266→                    text=True,
   267→                    bufsize=1,
   268→                )
   269→            except OSError as exc:
   270→                stop_live_writer.set()
   271→                writer_thread.join(timeout=2)
   272→                log_sections.append(f"{header}\n\nRUNNER ERROR:\n{exc}\n")
   273→                deps.safe_write_text_fn(log_file, "\n\n".join(log_sections))
   274→                return 127
   275→            except Exception as exc:  # pragma: no cover - defensive boundary
   276→                stop_live_writer.set()
   277→                writer_thread.join(timeout=2)
   278→                log_sections.append(f"{header}\n\nUNEXPECTED RUNNER ERROR:\n{exc}\n")
   279→                deps.safe_write_text_fn(log_file, "\n\n".join(log_sections))
   280→                return 1
   281→
   282→            def _drain_stream(stream, sink: list[str]) -> None:
   283→                if stream is None:
   284→                    return
   285→                try:
   286→                    for chunk in iter(stream.readline, ""):
   287→                        if not chunk:
   288→                            break
   289→                        with state_lock:
   290→                            sink.append(chunk)
   291→                            last_stream_activity[0] = time.monotonic()
   292→                except Exception as exc:  # pragma: no cover - defensive boundary
   293→                    with state_lock:
   294→                        sink.append(f"\n[stream read error: {exc}]\n")
   295→                finally:
   296→                    try:
   297→                        stream.close()
   298→                    except Exception:
   299→                        pass
   300→
   301→            stdout_thread = threading.Thread(
   302→                target=_drain_stream,
   303→                args=(process.stdout, stdout_chunks),
   304→                daemon=True,
   305→            )
   306→            stderr_thread = threading.Thread(
   307→                target=_drain_stream,
   308→                args=(process.stderr, stderr_chunks),
   309→                daemon=True,
   310→            )
   311→            stdout_thread.start()
   312→            stderr_thread.start()
   313→
   314→            while process.poll() is None:
   315→                now_monotonic = time.monotonic()
   316→                elapsed = int(max(0.0, now_monotonic - started_monotonic))
   317→                if elapsed >= deps.timeout_seconds:
   318→                    with state_lock:
   319→                        runner_note[:] = [f"timeout after {deps.timeout_seconds}s"]
   320→                    timed_out = True
   321→                    _terminate_process(process)
   322→                    break
   323→                if stall_after_output_seconds > 0:
   324→                    current_signature: tuple[int, int] | None = None
   325→                    try:
   326→                        stat = output_file.stat()
   327→                        current_signature = (int(stat.st_size), int(stat.st_mtime))
   328→                    except OSError:
   329→                        current_signature = None
   330→                    if current_signature is None:
   331→                        output_signature = None
   332→                        output_stable_since = None
   333→                    elif current_signature != output_signature:
   334→                        output_signature = current_signature
   335→                        output_stable_since = now_monotonic
   336→                    elif output_stable_since is not None:
   337→                        output_age = now_monotonic - output_stable_since
   338→                        stream_idle = now_monotonic - last_stream_activity[0]
   339→                        if (
   340→                            output_age >= stall_after_output_seconds
   341→                            and stream_idle >= stall_after_output_seconds
   342→                        ):
   343→                            with state_lock:
   344→                                runner_note[:] = [
   345→                                    (
   346→                                        "stall recovery triggered after "
   347→                                        f"{stall_after_output_seconds}s "
   348→                                        "with stable output file"
   349→                                    )
   350→                                ]
   351→                            recovered_from_stall = _output_file_has_json_payload()
   352→                            _terminate_process(process)
   353→                            break
   354→                deps.sleep_fn(min(live_log_interval_seconds, 1.0))
   355→
   356→            if process.poll() is None:
   357→                _terminate_process(process)
   358→            if stdout_thread is not None:
   359→                stdout_thread.join(timeout=2)
   360→            if stderr_thread is not None:
   361→                stderr_thread.join(timeout=2)
   362→            stop_live_writer.set()
   363→            writer_thread.join(timeout=2)
   364→            _write_live_snapshot()
   365→
   366→            stdout_text = "".join(stdout_chunks)
   367→            stderr_text = "".join(stderr_chunks)
   368→            if timed_out:
   369→                log_sections.append(
   370→                    f"{header}\n\nTIMEOUT after {deps.timeout_seconds}s\n\n"
   371→                    f"STDOUT:\n{stdout_text}\n\nSTDERR:\n{stderr_text}\n"
   372→                )
   373→                if _output_file_has_json_payload():
   374→                    log_sections.append(
   375→                        "Recovered timed-out batch from JSON output file; "
   376→                        "continuing as success."
   377→                    )
   378→                    deps.safe_write_text_fn(log_file, "\n\n".join(log_sections))
   379→                    return 0
   380→                deps.safe_write_text_fn(log_file, "\n\n".join(log_sections))
   381→                return 124
   382→            if recovered_from_stall:
   383→                log_sections.append(
   384→                    f"{header}\n\nSTALL RECOVERY after {stall_after_output_seconds}s "
   385→                    "of stable output and no stream activity.\n\n"
   386→                    f"STDOUT:\n{stdout_text}\n\nSTDERR:\n{stderr_text}\n"
   387→                )
   388→                if _output_file_has_json_payload():
   389→                    log_sections.append(
   390→                        "Recovered stalled batch from JSON output file; "
   391→                        "continuing as success."
   392→                    )
   393→                    deps.safe_write_text_fn(log_file, "\n\n".join(log_sections))
   394→                    return 0
   395→                deps.safe_write_text_fn(log_file, "\n\n".join(log_sections))
   396→                return 124
   397→
   398→            code = int(process.returncode or 0)
   399→            log_sections.append(
   400→                f"{header}\n\nSTDOUT:\n{stdout_text}\n\nSTDERR:\n{stderr_text}\n"
   401→            )
   402→        else:
   403→            # Compatibility path used by tests/injected runners.
   404→            writer_thread = _start_live_writer()
   405→            try:
   406→                result = deps.subprocess_run(
   407→                    cmd,
   408→                    capture_output=True,
   409→                    text=True,
   410→                    timeout=deps.timeout_seconds,
   411→                )
   412→            except deps.timeout_error as exc:
   413→                stop_live_writer.set()
   414→                writer_thread.join(timeout=2)
   415→                log_sections.append(
   416→                    f"{header}\n\nTIMEOUT after {deps.timeout_seconds}s\n{exc}\n"
   417→                )
   418→                deps.safe_write_text_fn(log_file, "\n\n".join(log_sections))
   419→                return 124
   420→            except OSError as exc:
   421→                stop_live_writer.set()
   422→                writer_thread.join(timeout=2)
   423→                log_sections.append(f"{header}\n\nRUNNER ERROR:\n{exc}\n")
   424→                deps.safe_write_text_fn(log_file, "\n\n".join(log_sections))
   425→                return 127
   426→            except (RuntimeError, ValueError) as exc:  # pragma: no cover - defensive boundary
   427→                stop_live_writer.set()
   428→                writer_thread.join(timeout=2)
   429→                log_sections.append(f"{header}\n\nUNEXPECTED RUNNER ERROR:\n{exc}\n")
   430→                deps.safe_write_text_fn(log_file, "\n\n".join(log_sections))
   431→                return 1
   432→            finally:
   433→                stop_live_writer.set()
   434→                writer_thread.join(timeout=2)
   435→
   436→            stdout_text = result.stdout or ""
   437→            stderr_text = result.stderr or ""
   438→            log_sections.append(
   439→                f"{header}\n\nSTDOUT:\n{stdout_text}\n\nSTDERR:\n{stderr_text}\n"
   440→            )
   441→            code = int(result.returncode)
   442→
   443→        if code == 0:
   444→            deps.safe_write_text_fn(log_file, "\n\n".join(log_sections))
   445→            return 0
   446→
   447→        combined = f"{stdout_text}\n{stderr_text}".lower()
   448→        is_transient = any(needle in combined for needle in _TRANSIENT_RUNNER_PHRASES)
   449→        if not is_transient or attempt >= max_attempts:
   450→            deps.safe_write_text_fn(log_file, "\n\n".join(log_sections))
   451→            return code
   452→
   453→        delay_seconds = retry_backoff_seconds * (2 ** (attempt - 1))
   454→        log_sections.append(
   455→            "Transient runner failure detected; "
   456→            f"retrying in {delay_seconds:.1f}s (attempt {attempt + 1}/{max_attempts})."
   457→        )
   458→        try:
   459→            if delay_seconds > 0:
   460→                deps.sleep_fn(delay_seconds)
   461→        except Exception:  # pragma: no cover - defensive boundary
   462→            # Retry should proceed even if delay hook fails.
   463→            pass
   464→
   465→    deps.safe_write_text_fn(log_file, "\n\n".join(log_sections))
   466→    return 1
   467→
   468→
   469→def run_followup_scan(
   470→    *,
   471→    lang_name: str,
   472→    scan_path: str,
   473→    deps: FollowupScanDeps,
   474→) -> int:
   475→    """Run a follow-up scan and return a non-zero status when it fails."""
   476→    scan_cmd = [
   477→        deps.python_executable,
   478→        "-m",
   479→        "desloppify",
   480→        "--lang",
   481→        lang_name,
   482→        "scan",
   483→        "--path",
   484→        scan_path,
   485→    ]
   486→    print(deps.colorize_fn("\n  Running follow-up scan...", "bold"))
   487→    try:
   488→        result = deps.subprocess_run(
   489→            scan_cmd,
   490→            cwd=str(deps.project_root),
   491→            timeout=deps.timeout_seconds,
   492→        )
   493→    except deps.timeout_error:
   494→        print(
   495→            deps.colorize_fn(
   496→                f"  Follow-up scan timed out after {deps.timeout_seconds}s.",
   497→                "yellow",
   498→            ),
   499→            file=sys.stderr,
   500→        )
   501→        return 124
   502→    except OSError as exc:
   503→        print(
   504→            deps.colorize_fn(f"  Follow-up scan failed: {exc}", "red"),
   505→            file=sys.stderr,
   506→        )
   507→        return 1
   508→    return int(getattr(result, "returncode", 0) or 0)
   509→
   510→
   511→def write_packet_snapshot(
   512→    packet: dict,
   513→    *,
   514→    stamp: str,
   515→    review_packet_dir: Path,
   516→    blind_path: Path,
   517→    safe_write_text_fn,
   518→) -> tuple[Path, Path]:
   519→    """Persist immutable and blind packet snapshots for runner workflows."""
   520→    review_packet_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   521→    packet_path = review_packet_dir / f"holistic_packet_{stamp}.json"
   522→    safe_write_text_fn(packet_path, json.dumps(packet, indent=2) + "\n")
   523→    blind_packet = _build_blind_packet(packet)
   524→    safe_write_text_fn(blind_path, json.dumps(blind_packet, indent=2) + "\n")
   525→    return packet_path, blind_path
   526→
   527→
   528→def _build_blind_packet(packet: dict) -> dict:
   529→    """Return a blind-review packet with score anchoring metadata removed."""
   530→    blind = deepcopy(packet)
   531→    for key in _BLIND_PACKET_DROP_KEYS:
   532→        blind.pop(key, None)
   533→
   534→    config = blind.get("config")
   535→    if isinstance(config, dict):
   536→        sanitized = _sanitize_blind_config(config)
   537→        if sanitized:
   538→            blind["config"] = sanitized
   539→        else:
   540→            blind.pop("config", None)
   541→    return blind
   542→
   543→
   544→def build_blind_packet(packet: dict) -> dict:
   545→    """Public wrapper for blind packet sanitization."""
   546→    return _build_blind_packet(packet)
   547→
   548→
   549→def _sanitize_blind_config(config: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
   550→    """Drop score/target hints from config while preserving unrelated options."""
   551→    sanitized: dict[str, Any] = {}
   552→    for key, value in config.items():
   553→        lowered = key.strip().lower()
   554→        if not lowered:
   555→            continue
   556→        if lowered in _BLIND_CONFIG_SCORE_HINT_KEYS:
   557→            continue
   558→        if "target" in lowered:
   559→            continue
   560→        if lowered.endswith("_score"):
   561→            continue
   562→        sanitized[key] = value
   563→    return sanitized
   564→
   565→
   566→def sha256_file(path: Path) -> str | None:
   567→    """Compute sha256 hex digest for path contents (or None on read failure)."""
   568→    try:
   569→        data = path.read_bytes()
   570→    except OSError:
   571→        return None
   572→    return sha256(data).hexdigest()
   573→
   574→
   575→def build_batch_import_provenance(
   576→    *,
   577→    runner: str,
   578→    blind_packet_path: Path,
   579→    run_stamp: str,
   580→    batch_indexes: list[int],
   581→) -> dict[str, Any]:
   582→    """Build provenance payload used to trust assessment-bearing imports."""
   583→    packet_hash = sha256_file(blind_packet_path)
   584→    batch_indexes_1 = sorted({int(index) + 1 for index in batch_indexes})
   585→    return {
   586→        "kind": "blind_review_batch_import",
   587→        "blind": True,
   588→        "runner": runner,
   589→        "run_stamp": run_stamp,
   590→        "created_at": datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
   591→        "batch_count": len(batch_indexes_1),
   592→        "batch_indexes": batch_indexes_1,
   593→        "packet_path": str(blind_packet_path),
   594→        "packet_sha256": packet_hash,
   595→    }
   596→
   597→
   598→def selected_batch_indexes(
   599→    *,
   600→    raw_selection: str | None,
   601→    batch_count: int,
   602→    parse_fn,
   603→    colorize_fn,
   604→) -> list[int]:
   605→    """Validate selected batch indexes or exit with a CLI error."""
   606→    try:
   607→        selected = parse_fn(raw_selection, batch_count)
   608→    except ValueError as exc:
   609→        print(colorize_fn(f"  Error: {exc}", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   610→        sys.exit(2)
   611→    if selected:
   612→        return selected
   613→    print(colorize_fn("  Error: no batches selected", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   614→    sys.exit(2)
   615→
   616→
   617→def prepare_run_artifacts(
   618→    *,
   619→    stamp: str,
   620→    selected_indexes: list[int],
   621→    batches: list[dict],
   622→    packet_path: Path,
   623→    run_root: Path,
   624→    repo_root: Path,
   625→    build_prompt_fn,
   626→    safe_write_text_fn,
   627→    colorize_fn,
   628→) -> tuple[Path, Path, dict[int, Path], dict[int, Path], dict[int, Path]]:
   629→    """Build prompt/output/log paths and persist prompts for selected batches."""
   630→    run_dir = run_root / stamp
   631→    prompts_dir = run_dir / "prompts"
   632→    results_dir = run_dir / "results"
   633→    logs_dir = run_dir / "logs"
   634→    prompts_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   635→    results_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   636→    logs_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   637→
   638→    selected_1_based = [idx + 1 for idx in selected_indexes]
   639→    print(colorize_fn(f"\n  Running holistic batches: {selected_1_based}", "bold"))
   640→    print(colorize_fn(f"  Run artifacts: {run_dir}", "dim"))
   641→
   642→    prompt_files: dict[int, Path] = {}
   643→    output_files: dict[int, Path] = {}
   644→    log_files: dict[int, Path] = {}
   645→    for idx in selected_indexes:
   646→        batch = batches[idx] if isinstance(batches[idx], dict) else {}
   647→        prompt_text = build_prompt_fn(
   648→            repo_root=repo_root,
   649→            packet_path=packet_path,
   650→            batch_index=idx,
   651→            batch=batch,
   652→        )
   653→        prompt_file = prompts_dir / f"batch-{idx + 1}.md"
   654→        output_file = results_dir / f"batch-{idx + 1}.raw.txt"
   655→        log_file = logs_dir / f"batch-{idx + 1}.log"
   656→        safe_write_text_fn(prompt_file, prompt_text)
   657→        prompt_files[idx] = prompt_file
   658→        output_files[idx] = output_file
   659→        log_files[idx] = log_file
   660→    return run_dir, logs_dir, prompt_files, output_files, log_files
   661→
   662→
   663→def execute_batches(
   664→    *,
   665→    selected_indexes: list[int],
   666→    prompt_files: dict[int, Path],
   667→    output_files: dict[int, Path],
   668→    log_files: dict[int, Path],
   669→    run_parallel: bool,
   670→    run_batch_fn,
   671→    safe_write_text_fn,
   672→    progress_fn=None,
   673→    max_parallel_workers: int | None = None,
   674→    heartbeat_seconds: float | None = 15.0,
   675→    clock_fn=time.monotonic,
   676→) -> list[int]:
   677→    """Execute batch prompts and return failed index list."""
   678→
   679→    def _emit_progress(
   680→        batch_index: int,
   681→        event: str,
   682→        code: int | None = None,
   683→        *,
   684→        details: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
   685→    ) -> None:
   686→        if not callable(progress_fn):
   687→            return
   688→        payload = details or {}
   689→        try:
   690→            progress_fn(batch_index, event, code, **payload)
   691→        except TypeError:
   692→            try:
   693→                progress_fn(batch_index, event, code)
   694→            except Exception:
   695→                return
   696→        except Exception:
   697→            return
   698→
   699→    failures: list[int] = []
   700→    if run_parallel:
   701→        requested_workers = (
   702→            int(max_parallel_workers)
   703→            if isinstance(max_parallel_workers, int) and max_parallel_workers > 0
   704→            else 8
   705→        )
   706→        max_workers = max(1, min(len(selected_indexes), requested_workers))
   707→        heartbeat = (
   708→            float(heartbeat_seconds)
   709→            if isinstance(heartbeat_seconds, int | float) and heartbeat_seconds > 0
   710→            else None
   711→        )
   712→        started_at: dict[int, float] = {}
   713→        started_at_lock = threading.Lock()
   714→        with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=max_workers) as executor:
   715→            futures = {}
   716→            for idx in selected_indexes:
   717→                _emit_progress(
   718→                    idx,
   719→                    "queued",
   720→                    None,
   721→                    details={"max_workers": max_workers},
   722→                )
   723→
   724→                def _run_one(batch_index: int) -> int:
   725→                    with started_at_lock:
   726→                        started_at[batch_index] = float(clock_fn())
   727→                    _emit_progress(
   728→                        batch_index,
   729→                        "start",
   730→                        None,
   731→                        details={"max_workers": max_workers},
   732→                    )
   733→                    return run_batch_fn(
   734→                        prompt=prompt_files[batch_index].read_text(),
   735→                        output_file=output_files[batch_index],
   736→                        log_file=log_files[batch_index],
   737→                    )
   738→
   739→                future = executor.submit(_run_one, idx)
   740→                futures[future] = idx
   741→
   742→            pending = set(futures.keys())
   743→
   744→            def _handle_completed_future(future) -> None:
   745→                idx = futures[future]
   746→                with started_at_lock:
   747→                    started_at_value = started_at.get(idx, float(clock_fn()))
   748→                try:
   749→                    code = future.result()
   750→                except Exception as exc:  # future.result() can raise any exception from the batch runner
   751→                    safe_write_text_fn(log_files[idx], f"Runner exception:\n{exc}\n")
   752→                    failures.append(idx)
   753→                    _emit_progress(
   754→                        idx,
   755→                        "done",
   756→                        1,
   757→                        details={"elapsed_seconds": int(max(0.0, clock_fn() - started_at_value))},
   758→                    )
   759→                    return
   760→                if code != 0:
   761→                    failures.append(idx)
   762→                _emit_progress(
   763→                    idx,
   764→                    "done",
   765→                    code,
   766→                    details={"elapsed_seconds": int(max(0.0, clock_fn() - started_at_value))},
   767→                )
   768→
   769→            if heartbeat is None:
   770→                for future in as_completed(pending):
   771→                    pending.discard(future)
   772→                    _handle_completed_future(future)
   773→                return failures
   774→
   775→            while pending:
   776→                try:
   777→                    future = next(as_completed(pending, timeout=heartbeat))
   778→                except FuturesTimeoutError:
   779→                    with started_at_lock:
   780→                        active_indexes = sorted(
   781→                            futures[fut] for fut in pending if futures[fut] in started_at
   782→                        )
   783→                    active_index_set = set(active_indexes)
   784→                    queued_indexes = sorted(
   785→                        futures[fut] for fut in pending if futures[fut] not in active_index_set
   786→                    )
   787→                    elapsed_seconds = {
   788→                        idx: int(
   789→                            max(
   790→                                0.0,
   791→                                clock_fn() - started_at.get(idx, clock_fn()),
   792→                            )
   793→                        )
   794→                        for idx in active_indexes
   795→                    }
   796→                    _emit_progress(
   797→                        -1,
   798→                        "heartbeat",
   799→                        None,
   800→                        details={
   801→                            "active_batches": active_indexes,
   802→                            "queued_batches": queued_indexes,
   803→                            "elapsed_seconds": elapsed_seconds,
   804→                            "active_count": len(active_indexes),
   805→                            "queued_count": len(queued_indexes),
   806→                            "total_count": len(selected_indexes),
   807→                        },
   808→                    )
   809→                    continue
   810→                pending.discard(future)
   811→                _handle_completed_future(future)
   812→        return failures
   813→
   814→    for idx in selected_indexes:
   815→        started_at = float(clock_fn())
   816→        _emit_progress(idx, "start", None, details={"max_workers": 1})
   817→        code = run_batch_fn(
   818→            prompt=prompt_files[idx].read_text(),
   819→            output_file=output_files[idx],
   820→            log_file=log_files[idx],
   821→        )
   822→        if code != 0:
   823→            failures.append(idx)
   824→        _emit_progress(
   825→            idx,
   826→            "done",
   827→            code,
   828→            details={"elapsed_seconds": int(max(0.0, clock_fn() - started_at))},
   829→        )
   830→    return failures
   831→
   832→
   833→def collect_batch_results(
   834→    *,
   835→    selected_indexes: list[int],
   836→    failures: list[int],
   837→    output_files: dict[int, Path],
   838→    allowed_dims: set[str],
   839→    extract_payload_fn,
   840→    normalize_result_fn,
   841→) -> tuple[list[BatchResult], list[int]]:
   842→    """Parse and normalize batch outputs, preserving prior failures."""
   843→    def _extract_payload_from_log(idx: int, raw_path: Path) -> dict[str, object] | None:
   844→        log_path = raw_path.parent.parent / "logs" / f"batch-{idx + 1}.log"
   845→        if not log_path.exists():
   846→            return None
   847→        try:
   848→            log_text = log_path.read_text()
   849→        except OSError:
   850→            return None
   851→
   852→        # Prefer the final STDOUT section for the attempt; if that fails, scan the full log.
   853→        stdout_marker = "\nSTDOUT:\n"
   854→        stderr_marker = "\n\nSTDERR:\n"
   855→        stdout_start = log_text.rfind(stdout_marker)
   856→        if stdout_start == -1 and log_text.startswith("STDOUT:\n"):
   857→            stdout_start = 0
   858→            stdout_offset = len("STDOUT:\n")
   859→        elif stdout_start >= 0:
   860→            stdout_offset = len(stdout_marker)
   861→        else:
   862→            stdout_offset = 0
   863→        if stdout_start >= 0:
   864→            start_idx = stdout_start + stdout_offset
   865→            stdout_end = log_text.find(stderr_marker, start_idx)
   866→            stdout_text = (
   867→                log_text[start_idx:] if stdout_end == -1 else log_text[start_idx:stdout_end]
   868→            )
   869→            payload = extract_payload_fn(stdout_text)
   870→            if payload is not None:
   871→                return payload
   872→            # If the batch log has a concrete STDOUT section but it contains no parseable
   873→            # payload, do not fallback to parsing the whole log. Full logs include the
   874→            # prompt template (often with JSON examples), which can generate misleading
   875→            # decode errors and hide the true runner failure in STDERR.
   876→            return None
   877→
   878→        return extract_payload_fn(log_text)
   879→
   880→    batch_results: list[BatchResult] = []
   881→    failure_set = set(failures)
   882→    for idx in selected_indexes:
   883→        had_execution_failure = idx in failure_set
   884→        raw_path = output_files[idx]
   885→        payload = None
   886→        parsed_from_log = False
   887→        if raw_path.exists():
   888→            try:
   889→                payload = extract_payload_fn(raw_path.read_text())
   890→            except OSError:
   891→                payload = None
   892→        if payload is None:
   893→            payload = _extract_payload_from_log(idx, raw_path)
   894→            parsed_from_log = payload is not None
   895→        if payload is None:
   896→            failure_set.add(idx)
   897→            continue
   898→        if parsed_from_log:
   899→            try:
   900→                raw_path.write_text(json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   901→            except OSError:
   902→                pass
   903→        try:
   904→            assessments, findings, dimension_notes, quality = normalize_result_fn(
   905→                payload,
   906→                allowed_dims,
   907→            )
   908→        except ValueError:
   909→            failure_set.add(idx)
   910→            continue
   911→        if had_execution_failure:
   912→            failure_set.discard(idx)
   913→        batch_results.append(
   914→            BatchResult(
   915→                batch_index=idx + 1,
   916→                assessments=assessments,
   917→                dimension_notes=dimension_notes,
   918→                findings=findings,
   919→                quality=quality,
   920→            )
   921→        )
   922→    return batch_results, sorted(failure_set)
   923→
   924→
   925→def _classify_runner_failure(log_text: str) -> str:
   926→    """Classify batch failure type from log contents."""
   927→    text = log_text.lower()
   928→    if "timeout after" in text:
   929→        return "timeout"
   930→    if any(phrase in text for phrase in _TRANSIENT_RUNNER_PHRASES):
   931→        return "stream_disconnect"
   932→    if (
   933→        "codex not found" in text
   934→        or ("no such file or directory" in text and "$ codex " in text)
   935→        or ("errno 2" in text and "codex" in text)
   936→    ):
   937→        return "runner_missing"
   938→    if any(
   939→        phrase in text
   940→        for phrase in (
   941→            "not authenticated",
   942→            "authentication failed",
   943→            "unauthorized",
   944→            "forbidden",
   945→            "login required",
   946→            "please login",
   947→            "access token",
   948→        )
   949→    ):
   950→        return "runner_auth"
   951→    if "runner exception" in text:
   952→        return "runner_exception"
   953→    return "unknown"
   954→
   955→
   956→def _has_codex_backend_connectivity_issue(log_text: str) -> bool:
   957→    """Return True when logs indicate Codex backend URL is unreachable."""
   958→    text = log_text.lower()
   959→    if "error sending request for url" not in text:
   960→        return False
   961→    return (
   962→        _CODEX_BACKEND_PATH_HINT in text
   963→        or _CODEX_BACKEND_HOST_HINT in text
   964→        or "nodename nor servname provided" in text
   965→        or "name or service not known" in text
   966→        or "temporary failure in name resolution" in text
   967→    )
   968→
   969→
   970→def _summarize_failure_categories(*, failures: list[int], logs_dir: Path) -> dict[str, int]:
   971→    """Return counts by failure category for failed batches."""
   972→    categories: dict[str, int] = {}
   973→    for idx in sorted(set(failures)):
   974→        log_file = logs_dir / f"batch-{idx + 1}.log"
   975→        if not log_file.exists():
   976→            category = "missing_log"
   977→        else:
   978→            try:
   979→                category = _classify_runner_failure(log_file.read_text())
   980→            except OSError:
   981→                category = "log_read_error"
   982→        categories[category] = categories.get(category, 0) + 1
   983→    return categories
   984→
   985→
   986→def _runner_failure_hints(*, failures: list[int], logs_dir: Path) -> list[str]:
   987→    """Infer common runner environment failures from batch logs."""
   988→    hints: list[str] = []
   989→    for idx in sorted(set(failures)):
   990→        log_file = logs_dir / f"batch-{idx + 1}.log"
   991→        try:
   992→            raw = log_file.read_text()
   993→        except OSError:
   994→            continue
   995→        text = raw.lower()
   996→        if (
   997→            "codex not found" in text
   998→            or ("no such file or directory" in text and "$ codex " in text)
   999→            or ("errno 2" in text and "codex" in text)
  1000→        ):
  1001→            hint = (
  1002→                "codex CLI not found on PATH. Install Codex CLI and verify `codex --version`."
  1003→            )
  1004→            if hint not in hints:
  1005→                hints.append(hint)
  1006→        if any(
  1007→            phrase in text
  1008→            for phrase in (
  1009→                "not authenticated",
  1010→                "authentication failed",
  1011→                "unauthorized",
  1012→                "forbidden",
  1013→                "login required",
  1014→                "please login",
  1015→                "access token",
  1016→            )
  1017→        ):
  1018→            hint = "codex runner appears unauthenticated. Run `codex login` and retry."
  1019→            if hint not in hints:
  1020→                hints.append(hint)
  1021→        if any(phrase in text for phrase in _TRANSIENT_RUNNER_PHRASES):
  1022→            hint = (
  1023→                "Transient Codex connectivity issue detected. Retry with "
  1024→                "`--batch-max-retries 2 --batch-retry-backoff-seconds 2` and, if needed, "
  1025→                "lower concurrency via `--max-parallel-batches 1`."
  1026→            )
  1027→            if hint not in hints:
  1028→                hints.append(hint)
  1029→        if _has_codex_backend_connectivity_issue(text):
  1030→            hint = (
  1031→                "Codex runner cannot reach chatgpt.com backend from this environment. "
  1032→                "Check outbound HTTPS/DNS/proxy access, or use cloud fallback: "
  1033→                "`desloppify review --external-start --external-runner claude`."
  1034→            )
  1035→            if hint not in hints:
  1036→                hints.append(hint)
  1037→        if "failed to load skill" in text and "missing yaml frontmatter" in text:
  1038→            hint = (
  1039→                "Codex loaded an invalid local skill file. Fix/remove malformed "
  1040→                "SKILL.md entries under `~/.codex/skills` to reduce runner noise."
  1041→            )
  1042→            if hint not in hints:
  1043→                hints.append(hint)
  1044→    return hints
  1045→
  1046→
  1047→def _print_failures_report(
  1048→    *,
  1049→    failures: list[int],
  1050→    packet_path: Path,
  1051→    logs_dir: Path,
  1052→    colorize_fn,
  1053→) -> None:
  1054→    """Render retry guidance for failed batches."""
  1055→    failed_1 = sorted({idx + 1 for idx in failures})
  1056→    failed_csv = ",".join(str(i) for i in failed_1)
  1057→    print(colorize_fn(f"\n  Failed batches: {failed_1}", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
  1058→    categories = _summarize_failure_categories(failures=failures, logs_dir=logs_dir)
  1059→    if categories:
  1060→        labels = {
  1061→            "timeout": "timeout",
  1062→            "stream_disconnect": "stream disconnect",
  1063→            "runner_missing": "runner missing",
  1064→            "runner_auth": "runner auth",
  1065→            "runner_exception": "runner exception",
  1066→            "missing_log": "missing log",
  1067→            "log_read_error": "log read error",
  1068→            "unknown": "unknown",
  1069→        }
  1070→        category_segments = [
  1071→            f"{labels.get(name, name)}={count}"
  1072→            for name, count in sorted(categories.items())
  1073→        ]
  1074→        print(
  1075→            colorize_fn(
  1076→                f"  Failure categories: {', '.join(category_segments)}",
  1077→                "yellow",
  1078→            ),
  1079→            file=sys.stderr,
  1080→        )
  1081→        if categories.get("timeout", 0) > 0:
  1082→            print(
  1083→                colorize_fn(
  1084→                    "  Timeout tuning: lower concurrency with `--max-parallel-batches 1..3` "
  1085→                    "or increase `--batch-timeout-seconds` for long-running reviews.",
  1086→                    "yellow",
  1087→                ),
  1088→                file=sys.stderr,
  1089→            )
  1090→        if categories.get("stream_disconnect", 0) > 0:
  1091→            print(
  1092→                colorize_fn(
  1093→                    "  Connectivity tuning: enable retries with `--batch-max-retries 2` "
  1094→                    "and `--batch-retry-backoff-seconds 2`, then retry failed batches.",
  1095→                    "yellow",
  1096→                ),
  1097→                file=sys.stderr,
  1098→            )
  1099→    print(colorize_fn("  Retry command:", "yellow"), file=sys.stderr)
  1100→    print(
  1101→        colorize_fn(
  1102→            f"    desloppify review --run-batches --packet {packet_path} --only-batches {failed_csv}",
  1103→            "yellow",
  1104→        ),
  1105→        file=sys.stderr,
  1106→    )
  1107→    for idx_1 in failed_1:
  1108→        log_file = logs_dir / f"batch-{idx_1}.log"
  1109→        print(colorize_fn(f"    log: {log_file}", "dim"), file=sys.stderr)
  1110→    hints = _runner_failure_hints(failures=failures, logs_dir=logs_dir)
  1111→    if hints:
  1112→        print(colorize_fn("  Environment hints:", "yellow"), file=sys.stderr)
  1113→        for hint in hints:
  1114→            print(colorize_fn(f"    {hint}", "dim"), file=sys.stderr)
  1115→
  1116→
  1117→def print_failures(
  1118→    *,
  1119→    failures: list[int],
  1120→    packet_path: Path,
  1121→    logs_dir: Path,
  1122→    colorize_fn,
  1123→) -> None:
  1124→    """Render retry guidance for failed batches without exiting."""
  1125→    _print_failures_report(
  1126→        failures=failures,
  1127→        packet_path=packet_path,
  1128→        logs_dir=logs_dir,
  1129→        colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
  1130→    )
  1131→
  1132→
  1133→def print_failures_and_exit(
  1134→    *,
  1135→    failures: list[int],
  1136→    packet_path: Path,
  1137→    logs_dir: Path,
  1138→    colorize_fn,
  1139→) -> None:
  1140→    """Render retry guidance for failed batches and exit non-zero."""
  1141→    _print_failures_report(
  1142→        failures=failures,
  1143→        packet_path=packet_path,
  1144→        logs_dir=logs_dir,
  1145→        colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
  1146→    )
  1147→    sys.exit(1)
  1148→
  1149→
  1150→__all__ = [
  1151→    "BatchResult",
  1152→    "CodexBatchRunnerDeps",
  1153→    "FollowupScanDeps",

  1155→    "build_blind_packet",
  1156→    "sha256_file",
  1157→    "codex_batch_command",
  1158→    "collect_batch_results",
  1159→    "execute_batches",
  1160→    "prepare_run_artifacts",
  1161→    "print_failures",
  1162→    "print_failures_and_exit",
  1163→    "run_codex_batch",
  1164→    "run_followup_scan",
  1165→    "run_stamp",
  1166→    "selected_batch_indexes",
  1167→    "write_packet_snapshot",
  1168→]
```
